\displaystyle\|XR-X_{2}\|\leq\|XR-X^{*}\|+\|X_{2}-X^{*}\|\leq(c_{3}+c_{4})\sqrt{n},

\displaystyle\|YR-Y_{2}\|\leq\|YR-Y^{*}\|+\|Y_{2}-Y^{*}\|\leq(c_{3}+c_{4})\sqrt{n}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{1}|\leq 10(c_{3}+c_{4})\sqrt{n\sigma_{\max}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|^{2}_{F}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|^{2}_{F}\right).

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\left\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\Delta_{X}Y^{T}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|X\Delta_{Y}^{T}\right\|_{F}^{2}-|\mathcal{E}_{1}|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{4}\left(\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)-|\mathcal{E}_{1}|

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{4}-10(c_{3}+c_{4})\sqrt{n\sigma_{\max}}\right)\left(\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right).

Here we use Weyl’s inequality to obtain that

\displaystyle|\sigma_{\min}(X)-\sigma_{\min}(X^{*})|\leq\|XR-X^{*}\|\leq c_{3}\sqrt{n},\ |\sigma_{\min}(Y)-\sigma_{\min}(Y^{*})|\leq\|YR-Y^{*}\|\leq c_{3}\sqrt{n}.

As long as
c_{3}\sqrt{n}\ll\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(X)\geq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}},\ \sigma_{\min}(Y)\geq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
B.1
.

According to the definition of
\nabla^{2}L(H,X,Y)
, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\nabla^{2}L(H,X,Y){\sf vec}(\Delta)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}(H,X,Y){\sf vec}(\Delta)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\nabla^{2}L(H,X,Y){\sf vec}(\Delta)+\lambda\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\lambda\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}+\lambda\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\lambda\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle.

(25)

We first deal with the last term, which is the only term that contains
A_{ij}
and thus has randomness. Note that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle.

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}\right)(\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}|P_{ij}-P_{ij}^{*}||(\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}|

(
mean-value theorem
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}(P_{ij}-P_{ij}^{*})^{2}}.

(
Cauchy
)

Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}(P_{ij}-P_{ij}^{*})^{2}\leq\sum_{i,j}(P_{ij}-P_{ij}^{*})^{2}=\sum_{i,j}\left(\langle H-H^{*},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle XY^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|Z(H-H^{*})Z^{T}+\frac{1}{n}(XY^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T})\right\|_{F}^{2}=\left\|Z(H-H^{*})Z^{T}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{1}{n}(XY^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T})\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\overline{c}\|H-H^{*}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|XY^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T}\|^{2}_{F},

(26)

where the last equation follows from the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{Z}(XY^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T})=0
and
\mathcal{P}_{Z}(Z(H-H^{*})Z^{T})=Z(H-H^{*})Z^{T}
.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}\cdot\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|H-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|XY^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T}\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}}\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}\cdot\left(c_{2}\sqrt{\overline{c}}+\frac{3c_{3}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}{n}\right)

(By the observations)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}

(as long as
c_{2}\sqrt{\overline{c}}+\frac{3c_{3}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}{n}\leq c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{2}}
for some
c
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

(
ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
)

For the second term, note that
(\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij})(\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}
is mean-zero
|(\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}|^{2}
-subgaussian variable. By the independency, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)(\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}

is mean-zero
\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|^{2}_{F}
-subgaussian variable. Thus, with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)(\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}\right|\lesssim\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}\sqrt{\log n}.

As a result, with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\|\Delta_{X}\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|_{F}

(as long as
\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}\ll\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{2}}
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right).

(
ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
)

To summarize, we show that with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y}\rangle\right|\leq c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right).

For the rest of the terms, note that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\|Z\Delta_{H}Z^{T}+\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\overline{c}}{4}\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\overline{c}\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\overline{c}\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{16\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|^{2}_{F}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|^{2}_{F}\right),

(27)

where the second inequality follows from Assumption
3
, the third inequality follows from the fact that
8c_{{\sf aug}}\leq 1
and the last inequality follows from Lemma
E.2
. Combine this with (
25
), we final obtain that with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}(H,X,Y){\sf vec}(\Delta)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\overline{c}\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\left(\frac{16\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}+\lambda+c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\right)\left(\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\overline{c}\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{20\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}\left(\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

as long as
\lambda,c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\ll\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}
. In other words, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}(H,X,Y)\right\|\leq\max\left\{\overline{c},\frac{20\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}\right\}.

It’s easy to see that the above upper bound also holds for
\|\nabla^{2}f(H,X,Y)\|
.

Now let’s focus on the lower bond. One can see that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\sum_{i,j}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{j}+\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ij}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i}+\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ii}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\left\|Z\Delta_{H}Z^{T}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})\right\|_{F}^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i}+\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ii}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\underline{c}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})\right\|_{F}^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i}+\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ii}\right)^{2}\right).

(28)

Here the last equation follows from the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{Z}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})=0
and
\mathcal{P}_{Z}(Z\Delta_{H}Z^{T})=Z\Delta_{H}Z^{T}
, and the inequality follows from Assumption
3
. On the one hand, one can control the last term as

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i}+\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})_{ii}\right)^{2}\leq 9\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i}\right)^{2}+\frac{\left(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}\right)_{ii}^{2}+\left(X\Delta_{Y}^{T}\right)_{ii}^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left\|z_{i}\right\|_{2}^{4}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\left\|(\Delta_{X})_{i,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|(Y)_{i,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|(\Delta_{Y})_{i,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|(X)_{i,:}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{c_{z}^{2}}{n^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|Y\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|X\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}}{n^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{c_{z}^{2}}{n}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|Y^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|X^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}}{n^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|^{2}_{F}}{n}

\displaystyle\ll

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}}{100}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{100n^{2}}\left(\left\|\Delta_{X}\right\|^{2}_{F}+\left\|\Delta_{Y}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right).

(29)

On the other hand, by Lemma
E.2
we have

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T})\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{4n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{4}-10(c_{3}+c_{4})\sqrt{n\sigma_{\max}}\right)\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}\sigma_{\min}}{8n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

(30)

as long as
\sigma_{\min}\geq 80(c_{3}+c_{4})\sqrt{n\sigma_{\max}}
. Combine (
29
) and (
30
) with (
28
) we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{X}Y^{T}+X\Delta_{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}+\frac{2c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{X}^{T}X+X^{T}\Delta_{X}-\Delta_{Y}^{T}Y-Y^{T}\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{2c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{e^{2c_{P}}\sigma_{\min}}{8n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)-\frac{e^{2c_{P}}\sigma_{\min}}{100n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{2c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{e^{2c_{P}}\sigma_{\min}}{10n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right).

Plugging this in (
25
) we get

\displaystyle{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}(H,X,Y){\sf vec}(\Delta)\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{2c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{e^{2c_{P}}\sigma_{\min}}{10n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-c\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n^{5/2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{2c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{e^{2c_{P}}\sigma_{\min}}{20n^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\underline{C}\left(\|\Delta_{H}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

as long as
n\gg c^{2}.

∎

Appendix F
Proofs of Section
C

We define

\displaystyle f_{{\sf diff}}(X,Y):=\frac{c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|X^{T}X-Y^{T}Y\|^{2}_{F}.

Thus, we have
f_{{\sf aug}}(H,X,Y)=f(H,X,Y)+f_{{\sf diff}}(X,Y)
.
Note that for any
H
,
F
, and
R\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}
, we have

\displaystyle f(H,FR)=f(H,F),\ \nabla_{H}f(H,FR)=\nabla_{H}f(H,F),\ \nabla_{F}f(H,FR)=\nabla_{F}f(H,F)R,

which will be used in the following proofs.
We first present the following lemmas, which will be constantly used in the proofs.

Lemma F.1
.

Let
H^{*},F^{*}
be the ground truth parameters and
\lambda\gtrsim\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}
. Under Assumption
2
, it holds with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
that

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n},\quad\|\nabla_{F}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}\lesssim\lambda\left(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\right).

Proof of Lemma
F.1
.

In the following, we will bound
\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}
and
\|\nabla_{F}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}
, respectively.
To bound
\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}
, note that

\displaystyle\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})=\nabla_{H}L(H^{*},F^{*})=\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)z_{i}z_{j}^{T}.

By Assumption
2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)^{2}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}z_{j}z_{i}^{T}\right]\right\|\lesssim\sum_{i\neq j}\|z_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\|z_{j}\|_{2}^{2}\asymp c_{z}^{2}.

Thus, by matrix Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\right\|\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{\log n},

which implies that
\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{p}\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n}
.

We then bound
\|\nabla_{F}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}
. Note that

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{F}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{X}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}+\|\nabla_{Y}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{X}L(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}+\|\nabla_{Y}L(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}+\lambda\left(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})\|\left(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\right)+\lambda\left(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\right),

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
\|AB\|_{F}\leq\|A\|\|B\|_{F}
. Here

\displaystyle\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}.

Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)^{2}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\right]\right\|\lesssim n\left\|\sum^{n}_{i=1}e_{i}e_{i}^{T}\right\|=n.

Thus, by matrix Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
, we have
\|\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}\lesssim\lambda
. Consequently, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{F}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}\lesssim\lambda\left(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\right).

∎

Lemma F.2
.

Suppose Lemma
C.1
holds for the
t
-th iteration. Under Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|H^{t}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|_{F}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n}{n}}.

Proof of Lemma
F.2
.

We denote

\displaystyle D^{t}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}.

Note that

\displaystyle\|D^{t}\|\leq\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|+\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|.

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}\left(P^{t}_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}\right)^{2}}

(by mean value theorem)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|H^{t}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|_{F}\right),

where the last inequality follows from the same argument as (
26
) and

\displaystyle\|X^{t}Y^{tT}-X^{*}Y^{*T}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|(X^{t}R^{t}-X^{*})(Y^{t}R^{t})^{T}+X^{*}(Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*})^{T}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|X^{t}R^{t}-X^{*}\|_{F}\|Y^{t}R^{t}\|+\|X^{*}\|\|Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|X^{t}R^{t}-X^{*}\|_{F}\|Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|+\|X^{t}R^{t}-X^{*}\|_{F}\|Y^{*}\|+\|X^{*}\|\|Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|X^{*}\|\|F^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|_{F}.

For the second term, as bound
\|\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})\|
in the proof of Lemma
F.1
, we have with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{t}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|H^{t}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|_{F}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}

(recall the definition of
\overline{C}
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n}{n}}.

We then finish the proof.
∎

F.1
Proofs of Lemma
C.1

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. In the following, we prove Lemma
C.1
for the
(t+1)
-th iteration.
By the gradient decent update, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}\\
F^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\\
F^{t}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t})\end{bmatrix},

which then gives

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{*}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}=\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{*}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}.

(31)

Consequently, we only need to bound the RHS of (
31
). Note that

\displaystyle{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle={\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}-\eta\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})

\displaystyle={\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}-\eta\left(\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{*},F^{*})\right)+\eta\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})-\eta\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{*},F^{*}).

Also, notice that

\displaystyle\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{*},F^{*})

\displaystyle=\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}\left((H^{*},F^{*})+\tau(H^{t}-H^{*},F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*})\right)d\tau\cdot{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left(I-\eta\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}\left((H^{*},F^{*})+\tau(H^{t}-H^{*},F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*})\right)d\tau\right)\cdot{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})-\eta\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{*},F^{*}).

For notation simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle A:=\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}\left((H^{*},F^{*})+\tau(H^{t}-H^{*},F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*})\right)d\tau.

Since Lemma
C.4
holds for the
t
-th iteration, we know
A
satisfies the local geometry properties as outlined in Lemma
B.1
as long as
c_{11}\leq c_{2}
,
c_{41}\leq c_{3}
.
By triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\|(I-\eta A){\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}}_{(1)}+\eta\underbrace{\|\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\|_{2}}_{(2)}+\eta\underbrace{\|\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{2}}_{(3)}.

In the following, we bound (1)-(3), respectively.

1.

We first bound (1).
Note that

\displaystyle(1)^{2}

\displaystyle={\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{T}(I-2\eta A+\eta^{2}A^{2}){\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\eta{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{T}A\ {\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{T}A^{2}\ {\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}.

By Lemma
B.1
, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{T}A\ {\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\geq\underline{C}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}^{2},

\displaystyle{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{T}A^{2}\ {\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\leq\overline{C}^{2}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}^{2},

where the second inequality holds since by Lemma
B.1
, we know
\|A\|\leq\overline{C}
. As a result, we have

\displaystyle(1)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\eta^{2}\overline{C}^{2}-2\underline{C}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\underline{C}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}^{2},

where the second inequality holds as long as
\eta\overline{C}^{2}\leq\underline{C}
.
This implies

\displaystyle(1)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F},

which follows from the fact that
\sqrt{1-x}\leq 1-\frac{x}{2}
.

2.

We then bound (2). Note that

\displaystyle\nabla_{X}f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})=\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}X^{t}(X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t})R^{t},

\displaystyle\nabla_{Y}f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})=\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}Y^{t}(Y^{tT}Y^{t}-X^{tT}X^{t})R^{t}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\nabla_{X}f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\\
\nabla_{Y}f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\nabla_{X}f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\\
\nabla_{Y}f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\left(\|X^{t}(X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t})R^{t}\|_{F}+\|Y^{t}(Y^{tT}Y^{t}-X^{tT}X^{t})R^{t}\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\left(\|X^{t}\|+\|Y^{t}\|\right)\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F}.

Since Lemma
C.1
holds for the
t
-th iteration, we have

\displaystyle\|F^{t}\|=\|F^{t}R^{t}\|\leq\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|+\|F^{*}\|\leq 2\|F^{*}\|,

where the last inequality holds because by Lemma
C.1
, we have
\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|\ll\|F^{*}\|
.
Consequently, we have

\displaystyle(2)=\|\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{t}R^{t})\|_{2}\leq\frac{16c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|F^{*}\|\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F}.

3.

We then bound (3).
Note that
X^{*T}X^{*}=Y^{*T}Y^{*}
. Thus, we have
\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(F^{*})=0
, which implies
\nabla f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{*},F^{*})=\nabla f(H^{*},F^{*})
.
By Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle(3)=\|\nabla f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{2}\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n}+\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}\lesssim\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}

as long as
c_{z}^{2}p\ll n
.

Consequently, we conclude that

\displaystyle\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}+\frac{16\eta c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|F^{*}\|\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F}+\lambda\eta\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}.

Recall (
31
), we then have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{*}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}+\frac{16\eta c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|F^{*}\|\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F}+\lambda\eta\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}.

By Lemma
J.1
, we know
\|F^{*}\|\leq 2\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}
. By Lemma
C.5
, we have
\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F}\leq c_{51}\eta n^{2}
.
We then obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{*}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{*}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}+{32c_{{\sf aug}}c_{51}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\eta^{2}}+\lambda\eta\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}.

Since Lemma
C.1
holds for the
t
-th iteration, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{*}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)c_{11}\sqrt{n}+{32c_{{\sf aug}}c_{51}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\eta^{2}}+\lambda\eta\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{11}\sqrt{n}

as long as
\lambda\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}\lesssim c_{11}\underline{C}
and
\frac{c_{51}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\eta}{\sqrt{n}}\lesssim c_{11}\underline{C}
.
Finally, by the definition of
R^{t+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{*}\|_{F}\leq\|F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{*}\|_{F}.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{*}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\leq c_{11}\sqrt{n}.

F.2
Proofs of Lemma
C.2

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. In the following, we prove Lemma
C.2
for the
(t+1)
-th iteration.
More specifically, we fix
m
and aim to bound

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1,(m)}-H^{t+1}\\
F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}.

Claim F.3
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\|F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}\|_{F}\leq\|F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\|_{F}.

Proof of Claim.

By the definition of
O^{t+1,(m)}
, for any
O\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}
, we have

\displaystyle\|F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}\|_{F}\leq\|F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O\|_{F}.

Choosing
O=O^{t,(m)}(R^{t})^{-1}R^{t+1}
, we then have

\displaystyle\|F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t,(m)}(R^{t})^{-1}R^{t+1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|F^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t,(m)}(R^{t})^{-1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\|_{F},

which then finishes the proofs.
∎

By Claim
F.3
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1,(m)}-H^{t+1}\\
F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\leq\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{t+1,(m)}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}.

Moreover, by the gradient decent update, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{t+1,(m)}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}\left(H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\right)-\left(H^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)\\
(F^{t}R^{t}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}))-(F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{F}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}))\end{bmatrix},

which further implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1,(m)}-H^{t+1}\\
F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}\left(H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\right)-\left(H^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)\\
(F^{t}R^{t}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}))-(F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{F}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}))\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}.

(32)

Thus, we only need to control the RHS of (
32
).

Notice that
\nabla_{H}f(H,F)=\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H,F)
and
\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H,F)=\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}_{{\sf aug}}(H,F)
, we have

\displaystyle H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})-\left(H^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle=H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t},F^{t})-\left(H^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle=H^{t}-H^{t,(m)}-\eta\left(\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t},F^{t})-\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left(\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})-\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle=H^{t}-H^{t,(m)}-\eta\left(\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\nabla_{H}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left(\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})-\nabla_{H}f(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right).

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle F^{t}R^{t}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-(F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{F}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}))

\displaystyle=F^{t}R^{t}-F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-\eta\left(\nabla_{F}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\nabla_{F}f_{{\sf aug}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left(\nabla_{F}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})-\nabla_{F}f(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left(\nabla_{F}f_{{\sf diff}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\nabla_{F}f_{{\sf diff}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right).

As a result, we have

\displaystyle{\sf vec}

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\left(H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\right)-\left(H^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)\\
(F^{t}R^{t}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}))-(F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{F}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}))\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left(I-\eta A\right){\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{t,(m)}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}}_{(1)}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\underbrace{\left(\nabla f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})-\nabla f(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right)}_{(2)}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\underbrace{\left(\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\nabla f_{{\sf diff}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right)}_{(3)}

where

\displaystyle A=\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f_{{\sf aug}}\left((H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})+\tau(H^{t}-H^{t,(m)},F^{t}R^{t}-F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right)d\tau.

In the following, we bound the Frobenius norm of (1)-(3), respectively.

1.

We first bound (1).
Since Lemma
C.2
and Lemma
C.4
hold for the
t
-th iteration, we have

\displaystyle\left\|H^{t,(m)}+\tau\left(H^{t}-H^{t,(m)}\right)-H^{*}\right\|_{F}\leq\|H^{t}-H^{*}\|_{F}+(1-\tau)\|H^{t,(m)}-H^{t}\|_{F}\leq c_{11}\sqrt{n}+c_{21}\leq c_{2}\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\left\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}+\tau\left(F^{t}R^{t}-F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\right)-F^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}+(1-\tau)\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}+\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}\leq c_{41}+c_{21}\leq c_{3}.

Thus Lemma
B.1
can be applied to bound the Frobenius norm of (1). Following the same argument as bounding term (1) in Appendix
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|(1)\|_{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{t,(m)}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}.

2.

We then bound (2).
Note that

\displaystyle(2)

\displaystyle=\nabla L^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})-\nabla L(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})

\displaystyle={\sf vec}\left(\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{m}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{m}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{m}e_{i}^{T}X^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}+\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}-A_{mi}\right)\begin{bmatrix}z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{m}^{T}X^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}\right).

Since the first and second terms are similar, we only focus on bounding the norm of the first term in the following.
Notice that

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)^{2}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{m}^{T}){\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{m}^{T})^{T}\right]\right\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)^{2}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{m}^{T})^{T}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{m}^{T})\right]\right\|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{i\neq m}\|{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{m}^{T})\|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{i\neq m}\|z_{i}z_{m}^{T}\|_{F}^{2}=\sum_{i\neq m}\|z_{i}\|^{2}_{2}\|z_{m}\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\frac{c_{z}^{2}}{n}.

Thus by matrix Berstein’s inequality, we have with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)z_{i}z_{m}^{T}\right\|_{F}=\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right){\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{m}^{T})\right\|_{2}\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}.

(33)

Notice that

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)^{2}{\sf vec}(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}){\sf vec}(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})^{T}\right]\right\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)^{2}{\sf vec}(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})^{T}{\sf vec}(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right]\right\|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{i\neq m}\|{\sf vec}(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{i\neq m}\|e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{i\neq m}{\sf Tr}\left(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}(Y^{t,(m)})^{T}e_{i}e_{m}^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{i\neq m}\left(Y^{t,(m)}(Y^{t,(m)})^{T}\right)_{ii}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|Y^{t,(m)}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq(\|Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-Y^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}+\|Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F})^{2}\lesssim\mu r\sigma_{\max},

where the last equation follows from Assumption
4
and the fact that Lemma
C.1
and Lemma
C.2
hold for the
t
-th iteration.
Thus by matrix Berstein’s inequality, we have with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right){\sf vec}(e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}}{n}.

(34)

Similarly, we have with probability at least
1-n^{-10}
that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}-A_{im}\right)e_{i}e_{m}^{T}X^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\right\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}}{n}.

(35)

Combine(
33
), (
34
) and (
35
), we conclude that
\|(2)\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}}{n}
.

3.

We then bound (3).
Notice that

\displaystyle\|F^{t,(m)}\|=\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\|\leq\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|+\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|+\|F^{*}\|\leq 2\|F^{*}\|.

Then following the same argument as bounding term (2) in Appendix
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{F}f_{{\sf diff}}(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|F^{*}\|\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{F}f_{{\sf diff}}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)})\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|F^{*}\|\|X^{t,(m)T}X^{t,(m)}-Y^{t,(m)T}Y^{t,(m)}\|_{F}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\|(3)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{{\sf aug}}}{n^{2}}\|F^{*}\|\left(\|X^{tT}X^{t}-Y^{tT}Y^{t}\|_{F}+\|X^{t,(m)T}X^{t,(m)}-Y^{t,(m)T}Y^{t,(m)}\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta c_{51}c_{{\sf aug}}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}.

Combine the bounds of Frobenius norm of (1)-(3), we conclude that

\displaystyle\left\|{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}\left(H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\right)-\left(H^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{H}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)})\right)\\
(F^{t}R^{t}-\eta\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}))-(F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-\eta\nabla_{F}f^{(m)}(H^{t,(m)},F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}))\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}-H^{t,(m)}\\
F^{t}R^{t}-F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}+c\eta\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}}{n}+c\eta^{2}c_{51}c_{{\sf aug}}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{C}}{2}\eta\right)c_{21}+c\eta\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}}{n}+c\eta^{2}c_{51}c_{{\sf aug}}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{21}

as long as
\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}}{\underline{C}n}\lesssim c_{21}
and
\eta\ll\frac{\underline{C}c_{21}}{c_{51}c_{{\sf aug}}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}
.
By (
32
), we further have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t+1}-H^{t+1,(m)}\\
F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\leq c_{21}.

Finally, by the arbitrariness of
m
, we finish the proofs.

F.3
Proofs of Lemma
C.3

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. In the following, we prove Lemma
C.3
for the
(t+1)
-th iteration. Note that

\displaystyle\nabla_{X}L^{(m)}(H,X,Y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
i,j\neq m\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}}}{1+e^{P_{im}}}-\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{m}^{T}Y+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{m}e_{i}^{T}Y,

where the
m
-th row of the first and second terms are all zeros. Thus, by the gradient descent update, we have

\displaystyle\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}

\displaystyle=\left(X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}-\eta\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}+\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}\right\}\right)R^{t+1,(m)}-X^{*}_{m,\cdot}

\displaystyle=\left\{X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}_{m,\cdot}-\eta\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}+\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}\right)R^{t,(m)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}-\eta\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}+\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}\right)R^{t,(m)}\right\}\left(\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right)

By the mean value theorem, we have for some
\{c_{i}\}
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P^{t,(m)}_{mi}}}{1+e^{P^{t,(m)}_{mi}}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{mi}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{mi}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(P^{t,(m)}_{mi}-P^{*}_{mi}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\langle H^{t,(m)}-H^{*},z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(X^{t,(m)}(Y^{t,(m)})^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T}\right)_{mi}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}.

Note that

\displaystyle X^{t,(m)}(Y^{t,(m)})^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T}

\displaystyle=\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)\left(Y^{*}\right)^{T}+(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}.

Thus, we further have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P^{t,(m)}_{mi}}}{1+e^{P^{t,(m)}_{mi}}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{mi}}}{1+e^{P^{*}_{mi}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\langle H^{t,(m)}-H^{*},z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left((X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}\right)_{mi}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}

\displaystyle=\left(I_{r}-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\langle H^{t,(m)}-H^{*},z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left((X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}\right)_{mi}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\eta\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}

\displaystyle+\left\{X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}-\eta\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}+\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}\right)R^{t,(m)}\right\}\left(\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(I_{r}-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})^{\top}\right)\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}+r_{1},

(36)

where

\displaystyle r_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\underbrace{\left(\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)_{i,\cdot}^{\top}\right)\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}}_{(a)}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta}{n}\underbrace{\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\langle H^{t,(m)}-H^{*},z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}}_{(b)}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\underbrace{\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left((X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}\right)_{mi}e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}}_{(c)}-\eta\lambda\underbrace{X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}}_{(d)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\{X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}-\eta\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}+\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}\right)R^{t,(m)}\right\}\left(\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right)}_{(e)}

We bound
\|r_{1}\|_{2}
in the following.

1.

For (a), by Cahuchy-Schwarz, we have

\displaystyle\|(a)\|_{2}\leq\left\|\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\|_{F}\|Y^{*}\|_{F}.

Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}\leq c_{31}

\displaystyle\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}+\|Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 5\kappa\|Y^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-Y^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}+\|Y^{t}R^{t}-Y^{*}\|_{F}

(by Lemma
J.3
)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 5\kappa c_{21}+c_{11}\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|(a)\|_{2}\lesssim c_{11}c_{31}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}n}.

(37)

2.

For (b), note that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\langle H^{t,(m)}-H^{*},z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)e_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq m}\left|\langle H^{t,(m)}-H^{*},z_{m}z_{i}^{T}\rangle\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\|H^{t,(m)}-H^{*}\|\|z_{m}\|_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq m}\|z_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{z}}{4\sqrt{n}}\|H^{t,(m)}-H^{*}\|.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|Y^{t,(m)}\|\leq\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|+\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|+\|F^{*}\|\leq 2\|F^{*}\|.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|(b)\|_{2}\leq\frac{c_{z}}{\sqrt{n}}\|H^{t,(m)}-H^{*}\|\|F^{*}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}c_{z}c_{11}.

(38)

3.

For (c), note that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left((X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}\right)_{mi}e_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left\|\left((X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\left\|(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})^{\top}_{m,\cdot}\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)^{T}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left\|(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)})_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|F^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right\|.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|(c)\|_{2}\lesssim\|F^{*}\|\left\|F^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\mu r}c_{11}\sigma_{\max}.

(39)

4.

For (d), we have

\displaystyle\|(d)\|_{2}\leq\left\|X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}.

(40)

5.

Finally, we bound (e).
We denote

\displaystyle(1)

\displaystyle:=\left\{X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}_{m,\cdot}-\eta\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{mi}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}^{T}Y^{t,(m)}+\lambda X^{t,(m)}_{m,\cdot}\right)R^{t,(m)}\right\}

\displaystyle=\left(I_{r}-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})^{\top}\right)\left(X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}(a)-\frac{\eta}{n}(b)-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}(c)-\eta\lambda(d).

Note that, by Cauchy-Schwartz, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})^{\top}\right\|\leq\|Y^{*}\|_{F}^{2}.

Thus, it can be seen that
\|(1)\|_{2}\leq\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}
and we have

\displaystyle\|(1)+X^{*}_{m,\cdot}\|_{2}\leq\|(1)\|_{2}+\|X^{*}_{m,\cdot}\|_{2}\leq\|(1)\|_{2}+\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}.

Note that

\displaystyle(e)=\left((1)+X^{*}_{m,\cdot}\right)\left(\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right).

Regarding the term
\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}
, we have the following claim.

Claim F.4
.

With probability at least
1-n^{-10}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right\|\lesssim\frac{\eta}{n}\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\|(e)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(1)+X^{*}_{m,\cdot}\|_{2}\left\|\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta}{n}\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta}{n}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}c_{11}.

(41)

It remains to prove Claim
F.4
.

Proof of Claim
F.4
.

To facilitate analysis, we introduce an auxiliary point
\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}:=\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{X}^{t+1,(m)}\\
\tilde{Y}^{t+1,(m)}\end{bmatrix}
where

\displaystyle\tilde{X}^{t+1,(m)}

\displaystyle={X}^{t,(m)}{R}^{t,(m)}-\eta\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
i,j\neq m\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}\right)(e_{i}e_{m}^{T}+e_{m}e_{i}^{T})\right]Y^{*}

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\lambda X^{*}-\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}\eta}{n^{2}}X^{*}({R}^{t,(m)})^{T}\left(({X}^{t,(m)})^{T}{X}^{t,(m)}-({Y}^{t,(m)})^{T}{Y}^{t,(m)}\right){R}^{t,(m)},

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}^{t+1,(m)}

\displaystyle={Y}^{t,(m)}{R}^{t,(m)}-\eta\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
i,j\neq m\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{j}e_{i}^{T}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}\right)(e_{i}e_{m}^{T}+e_{m}e_{i}^{T})\right]X^{*}

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\lambda Y^{*}-\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}\eta}{n^{2}}Y^{*}({R}^{t,(m)})^{T}\left(({Y}^{t,(m)})^{T}{Y}^{t,(m)}-({X}^{t,(m)})^{T}{X}^{t,(m)}\right){R}^{t,(m)}.

We have the following claim.

Claim F.5
.

It holds that

\displaystyle I_{r}={\arg\min}_{R\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}R-F^{*}\right\|_{F},\text{ and }\sigma_{\min}\left(\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)T}F^{*}\right)\geq\sigma_{\min}/2.

Proof of Claim
F.5
.

See Claim 4 in
Chen et al., (2020)
.
∎

With this claim at hand, by Lemma
J.5
with
S=\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)T}F^{*}
and
K=\left({F}^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}\right)^{T}F^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right\|

(42)

\displaystyle=\left\|\text{sgn}(S+K)-\text{sgn}(S)\right\|

(by Claim
F.5
and the definition of
R^{t+1,(m)}
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}\left(\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)T}F^{*}\right)}\left\|\left({F}^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}\right)^{T}F^{*}\right\|

(by Lemma
J.5
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}\left\|{F}^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}\right\|\|F^{*}\|.

(by Claim
F.5
)

Here
\text{sgn}(A)=UV^{\top}
for a matrix
A
with SVD
U\Sigma V^{\top}
. Note that

\displaystyle F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}

\displaystyle=-\eta\begin{bmatrix}B&0\\
0&B^{\top}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\\
X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}\eta}{n^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}X^{\star}\\
-Y^{\star}\end{bmatrix}R^{t,(m)\top}CR^{t,(m)}-\eta\lambda\begin{bmatrix}X^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-X^{*}\\
Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\end{bmatrix},

where we denote

\displaystyle B:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
i,j\neq m\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq m}\left(\frac{e^{P_{im}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{im}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{im}^{*}}}\right)(e_{i}e_{m}^{T}+e_{m}e_{i}^{T}),

\displaystyle C:=X^{t,(m)\top}X^{t,(m)}-Y^{t,(m)\top}Y^{t,(m)}.

This enables us to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\eta\left\|B\right\|\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|+\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}\eta}{n^{2}}\left\|F^{\star}\right\|\left\|C\right\|_{F}+\eta\lambda\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|.

We then bound
\|B\|
in the following.
Note that

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
i,j\neq m\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}.

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t,(m)}}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}\left(P_{ij}^{t,(m)}-P_{ij}^{*}\right)^{2}}

(by mean value theorem)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|H^{t,(m)}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|F^{*}\|\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\|_{F}.

For the second term, same as bounding
\|\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})\|
in the proof of Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
i,j\neq m\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\|B\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|H^{t,(m)}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|F^{*}\|\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\|_{F}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}}{n}}c_{11}+\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-\tilde{F}^{t+1,(m)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq c\eta\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}c^{2}_{11}+\log n}{n}}\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|+\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}\eta}{n^{2}}\left\|F^{\star}\right\|\left\|X^{t,(m)\top}X^{t,(m)}-Y^{t,(m)\top}Y^{t,(m)}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\lambda\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|.

By (
42
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left(R^{t,(m)}\right)^{-1}R^{t+1,(m)}-I_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\|F^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\Bigg(c\eta\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}c^{2}_{11}+\log n}{n}}\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|+\frac{4c_{{\sf aug}}\eta}{n^{2}}\left\|F^{\star}\right\|\left\|X^{t,(m)\top}X^{t,(m)}-Y^{t,(m)\top}Y^{t,(m)}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\eta\lambda\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|F^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\eta\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}c^{2}_{11}+\log n}{n}}\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|+c_{{\sf aug}}c_{51}\eta\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}+\lambda\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}{\sigma_{\min}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}c^{2}_{11}+\log n}{n}}+\lambda\right)\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|

(as long as
\eta
small enough)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\eta}{n}\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right\|

as long as
\frac{n\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}{\sigma_{\min}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\overline{C}c^{2}_{11}+\log n}{n}}+\lambda\right)\ll 1
.
We then prove Claim
F.4
.

∎

Combine (
37
), (
38
), (
39
), (
40
), (
41
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|r_{1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}c_{11}c_{31}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}+\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}c_{z}c_{11}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sqrt{\mu r}c_{11}\sigma_{\max}+\lambda\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}c_{11}\right).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}

\displaystyle=\left(I_{r}-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(X^{*}_{i,\cdot})(X^{*}_{i,\cdot})^{\top}\right)\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}+r_{2},

(43)

where

\displaystyle\|r_{2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}c_{11}c_{31}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}+\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}c_{z}c_{11}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sqrt{\mu r}c_{11}\sigma_{\max}+\lambda\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}c_{11}\right).

By(
36
) and (
43
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=A\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}r_{1}\\
0\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
r_{2}\end{bmatrix}

(44)

where

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}I_{r}-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})(Y^{*}_{i,\cdot})^{\top}&0\\
0&I_{r}-\frac{\eta}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}(X^{*}_{i,\cdot})(X^{*}_{i,\cdot})^{\top}\\
\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=I_{2r}-\eta\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}Y^{*}_{i,\cdot}\\
0\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\eta\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\frac{1}{n}X^{*}_{i,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle=I_{2r}-\eta\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}Y^{*}\\
0\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\eta\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle=I_{2r}-\eta\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}Y^{*}\\
0\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right).

Notice that
|P^{t,(m)}_{im}|\leq 2|P^{*}_{im}|
. Thus, by Assumption
2
, we have
|c_{i}|\leq 2|P^{*}_{im}|\leq 2c_{P}
, which implies

\displaystyle\frac{e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\leq\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

Denote

\displaystyle B:=\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}Y^{*}\\
0\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right).

We then have

\displaystyle\left\|A\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}(I-2\eta B+\eta^{2}B^{2})\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}_{2}-2\eta\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}B\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}B^{2}\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}

(45)

Denote

\displaystyle B_{1}:=\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2},\text{ and }B_{2}:=\sum_{i\neq m}\frac{e^{c_{i}}}{(1+e^{c_{i}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}^{\top}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}.

It can be seen that

\displaystyle B_{1}\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}Y^{*}_{i,\cdot}Y^{*T}_{i,\cdot}=\frac{1}{4n^{2}}Y^{*T}Y^{*}\preccurlyeq\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{4n^{2}}I_{2r},

\displaystyle B_{2}\preccurlyeq\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\sum^{n}_{i=1}X^{*}_{i,\cdot}X^{*T}_{i,\cdot}=\frac{1}{4n^{2}}X^{*T}X^{*}\preccurlyeq\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{4n^{2}}I_{2r}.

Thus,
\|B\|\leq\|B_{1}\|+\|B_{2}\|\leq\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{2n^{2}}
. On the other hand, we want to lower bound the smallest eigenvalue of
B
. For any
\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}
, where
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}B\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{x}}^{\top}B_{1}{\mathbf{x}}+{\mathbf{y}}^{\top}B_{2}{\mathbf{y}}.

(46)

An important observation is that
B_{1},B_{2}
are submatrices of
D^{*}
, a fact we will leverage in the subsequent proofs.
We denote by
v\in\mathbb{R}^{p^{2}+2nr}
such that

\displaystyle v_{k}=\begin{cases}x_{j}&\text{if }k=p^{2}+(m-1)r+j\text{ for some }j\in[r]\\
0&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}.

Then we know that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}B_{1}{\mathbf{x}}\geq\frac{4e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}v^{\top}D^{*}v\geq\frac{4e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}v^{\top}\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}v.

(47)

Denote by
Q:=(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}
, then Assumption
5
implies

\displaystyle v^{\top}\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}v\geq\underline{c}_{D^{*}}\left\|Qv\right\|_{2}^{2}.

(48)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\left\|Qv\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{j=1}^{r}(Qv)_{p^{2}+(m-1)r+j}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\left(v_{p^{2}+(m-1)r+j}-((I-Q)v)_{p^{2}+(m-1)r+j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{j=1}^{r}v_{p^{2}+(m-1)r+j}^{2}-2\left(\sum_{j=1}^{r}\left|v_{p^{2}+(m-1)r+j}\right|\right)\left\|(I-Q)v\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\geq\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{2}-2\sqrt{r}\left\|v\right\|_{2}\left\|I-Q\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|v\right\|_{2}\geq\left(1-2c_{2,\infty}\sqrt{\frac{r^{3}+rp}{n}}\right)\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{2}

according to Assumption
6
. Therefore, as long as
n\geq 16(r^{3}+rp)c_{2,\infty}^{2}
, we have
\left\|Qv\right\|_{2}^{2}\geq\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{2}/2
. Combine this with (
47
) and (
48
) we get

\displaystyle{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}B_{1}{\mathbf{x}}\geq\frac{4e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}{2}\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{y}}^{\top}B_{2}{\mathbf{y}}\geq\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{y}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Plugging these in (
46
) we know that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}B\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}\geq\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{x}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{y}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|^{2}_{2}.

Since this holds for all
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
, we know that

\displaystyle B\succcurlyeq\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}I_{2r}.

To sum up, as long as
n\geq 16(r^{3}+rp)c_{2,\infty}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}I_{2r}\preccurlyeq B\preccurlyeq\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{2n^{2}}I_{2r}.

By (
45
), we then have

\displaystyle\left\|A\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{4\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\eta+\frac{\sigma_{\max}^{2}}{4n^{4}}\eta^{2}\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\eta\right)\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}^{2}

as long as
\eta\leq\frac{8n^{2}e^{2c_{P}}\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}{\sigma_{\max}^{2}(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}
.
Recall equation (
44
). Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|A\begin{bmatrix}\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\\
\left(F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}+\|r_{1}\|_{2}+\|r_{2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}\eta\right)c_{31}

\displaystyle\quad+c\eta\left(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}c_{11}c_{31}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}+\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}c_{z}c_{11}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sqrt{\mu r}c_{11}\sigma_{\max}+\lambda\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}c_{11}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c_{31}

as long as
\max\{\frac{c_{z}}{n}\sqrt{\overline{C}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}c_{11},\lambda\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}\}\lesssim\frac{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{2c_{P}}}{(1+e^{2c_{P}})^{2}}c_{31}
.

F.4
Proofs of Lemma
C.4

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. In the following, we prove Lemma
C.4
for the
(t+1)
-th iteration.
Since Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th, we know Lemma
C.1
holds for the
t+1
-th iteration, which implies
\|H^{t+1}-H^{*}\|_{F}\leq c_{11}\sqrt{n}
.

For
1\leq m\leq n
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+5\kappa\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right\|_{F},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\left(F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+5\kappa\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right\|_{F},

where the last inequalities follow from Lemma
J.3
.
It then holds that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq m\leq n}\left\|\left(F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq m\leq n}\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+5\kappa\max_{1\leq m\leq n}\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c_{31}+5\kappa c_{21}=c_{41},

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq m\leq n}\left\|\left(F^{t+1}R^{t+1}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq m\leq n}\left\|\left(F^{t+1,(m)}R^{t+1,(m)}-F^{*}\right)_{m+n,\cdot}\right\|_{2}+5\kappa\max_{1\leq m\leq n}\left\|F^{t+1,(m)}O^{t+1,(m)}-F^{t+1}R^{t+1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c_{31}+5\kappa c_{21}=c_{41},

where the last inequalities hold since Lemma
C.2
and
C.3
hold for the
(t+1)
-th iteration.
We then finish the proof.

F.5
Proofs of Lemma
C.5

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. In the following, we prove Lemma
C.5
for the
(t+1)
-th iteration.

We first show
\|(X^{t+1})^{T}X^{t+1}-(Y^{t+1})^{T}Y^{t+1}\|_{F}\leq c_{51}\eta n^{2}
. We denote

\displaystyle A^{t}=(X^{t})^{T}X^{t}-(Y^{t})^{T}Y^{t},\ A^{t+1}=(X^{t+1})^{T}X^{t+1}-(Y^{t+1})^{T}Y^{t+1},

\displaystyle D^{t}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}-A_{ij}\right)e_{i}e_{j}^{T}.

Same as Lemma 15 in
Chen et al., (2020)
, it can be seen that

\displaystyle\|A^{t+1}\|_{F}\leq(1-\lambda\eta)\|A^{t}\|_{F}+\eta^{2}\|Y^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}Y^{t}-X^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}X^{t}\|_{F}.

(49)

Note that

\displaystyle\|Y^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}Y^{t}-X^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}X^{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|Y^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}Y^{t}\|_{F}+\|X^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}X^{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|Y^{t}\|\|D^{t}\|^{2}\|Y^{t}\|_{F}+\|X^{t}\|\|D^{t}\|^{2}\|X^{t}\|_{F}.

Moreover, by Lemma
C.1
for the
t
-th iteration, we have

\displaystyle\|X^{t}\|\leq\|X^{t}R^{t}-X^{*}\|+\|X^{*}\|\leq 2\|X^{*}\|,\quad\|X^{t}\|_{F}\leq\|X^{t}R^{t}-X^{*}\|_{F}+\|X^{*}\|_{F}\leq 2\|X^{*}\|_{F},

\displaystyle\|Y^{t}\|\leq 2\|Y^{*}\|,\ \|Y^{t}\|_{F}\leq 2\|Y^{*}\|_{F}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|Y^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}Y^{t}-X^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}X^{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 4\|Y^{*}\|\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\|D^{t}\|^{2}+4\|X^{*}\|\|X^{*}\|_{F}\|D^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma_{\max}\|D^{t}\|^{2}.

By Lemma
F.2
, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{t}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n}{n}}.

This implies

\displaystyle\|Y^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}Y^{t}-X^{tT}D^{tT}D^{t}X^{t}\|_{F}\lesssim\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma_{\max}\|D^{t}\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma_{\max}(c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n)}{n}.

(50)

Combine (
49
) and (
50
), we have

\displaystyle\|A^{t+1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda\eta)\|A^{t}\|_{F}+C\eta^{2}\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma_{\max}(c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n)}{n}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda\eta)c_{51}\eta n^{2}+C\eta^{2}\frac{\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma_{\max}(c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n)}{n}

\displaystyle\leq c_{51}\eta n^{2}

as long as
\lambda\eta<1
and
c_{51}\lambda n^{3}\gg\sqrt{\mu r}\sigma_{\max}(c_{11}^{2}\overline{C}+\log n)
.
The upper bound on the leave-one-out sequences can be derived similarly.

We then prove (
16
) in the following.
By the gradient descent update, we have

\displaystyle f(H^{t+1},F^{t+1})

\displaystyle=f(H^{t+1},F^{t+1}R^{t})

\displaystyle=f\left(H^{t}-\eta\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}),F^{t}R^{t}-\eta\begin{bmatrix}\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}&0\\
0&\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right)

\displaystyle=f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\eta\left\langle\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}),\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\left\langle\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}),\begin{bmatrix}\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}&0\\
0&\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta^{2}}{2}{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\\
\begin{bmatrix}\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}&0\\
0&\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F}){\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\\
\begin{bmatrix}\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}&0\\
0&\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\eta\left\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|_{F}^{2}-\eta\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}&0\\
0&\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\end{bmatrix}\nabla_{F}f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta^{2}}{2}\left(\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F})\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})

\displaystyle=f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})-\eta\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta^{2}}{2}\left(\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F})\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t}).

By Lemma
B.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F})\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\leq\overline{C}\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t}R^{t})\right\|^{2}_{2}=\overline{C}\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|^{2}_{2}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle f(H^{t+1},F^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq f(H^{t},F^{t})-\left(\eta-\frac{\overline{C}}{2}\eta^{2}\right)\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq f(H^{t},F^{t})-\frac{\eta}{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|^{2}_{2}

as long as
\overline{{C}}\eta\leq 1
. We then finish the proofs.

F.6
Proofs of Lemma
C.6

Proof.

Summing (
16
) from
t=1
to
t=t_{0}-1
leads to

\displaystyle f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})\leq f(H^{0},F^{0})-\frac{\eta}{2}\sum^{t_{0}-1}_{t=0}\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|^{2}_{2}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\min_{0\leq t<t_{0}}\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{t_{0}}\sum^{t_{0}-1}_{t=0}\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(H^{t},F^{t})\right\|^{2}_{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2}{\eta t_{0}}\left(f(H^{*},F^{*})-f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})\right)\right)^{1/2},

(51)

where we use the fact that
(H^{0},F^{0})=(H^{*},F^{*})
.
Thus, it remains to control
f(H^{*},F^{*})-f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})
. Note that

\displaystyle f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle=f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle=f(H^{*},F^{*})+\left\langle\nabla f(H^{*},F^{*}),{\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}{\sf vec}\left(\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F}){\sf vec}\left(\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right),

where
(\tilde{H},\tilde{F})
lies in the line segment connecting
(H^{*},F^{*})
and
(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}})
. By triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle f(H^{*},F^{*})-f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{H}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}\|H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\|\nabla_{F}f(H^{*},F^{*})\|_{F}\|F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\left|{\sf vec}\left(\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F}){\sf vec}\left(\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)\right|.

(52)

By Lemma
C.4
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}\leq\|{H}^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\|_{F}\leq c_{11}\sqrt{n}\leq c_{2}\sqrt{n},

\displaystyle\|\tilde{F}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|{F}^{t_{0}}{R}^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\|_{\infty}\leq c_{41}\leq c_{3}.

Thus, by Lemma
B.1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left|{\sf vec}\left(\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}f(\tilde{H},\tilde{F}){\sf vec}\left(\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\overline{C}}{2}\left\|\begin{bmatrix}H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\\
F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\overline{C}c_{11}^{2}n}{2},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
.

Consequently, by (
52
) and Lemma
F.1
, we have shown that

\displaystyle f(H^{*},F^{*})-f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n}\|H^{t_{0}}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}\|F^{t_{0}}R^{t_{0}}-F^{*}\|_{F}+\overline{C}c_{11}^{2}n,

where we use the fact that
\|X^{*}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}
by Assumption
4
.
By Lemma
C.1
, this further implies that

\displaystyle f(H^{*},F^{*})-f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle\lesssim(c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n}+\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}})\cdot c_{11}\sqrt{n}+\overline{C}c_{11}^{2}n

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{2}

as long as
(c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n}+\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}})\cdot c_{11}\sqrt{n}+\overline{C}c_{11}^{2}n\ll n^{2}
.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\eta t_{0}}\left(f(H^{*},F^{*})-f(H^{t_{0}},F^{t_{0}})\right)\lesssim\frac{n^{2}}{\eta t_{0}}\leq n^{-10}

as long as
\eta t_{0}\geq n^{12}
(which holds as long as
t_{0}
large enough). Together with (
51
), we finish the proofs.

∎

Appendix G
Proofs of Section
D

We first prove some useful lemmas in the following.

Lemma G.1
.

Under Assumption
5
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}

and

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}{2}\leq\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})\leq\lambda_{\max}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})\leq{2\overline{c}_{D^{*}}}.

Here

\displaystyle\hat{c}=\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\left(c_{z}c_{11}\overline{c}_{D^{*}}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n}(c_{41}\overline{c}_{D^{*}}+c_{11})\right).

Proof of Lemma
G.1
.

We first control
\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|
in the following.
Note that

\displaystyle\hat{D}-D^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\right)\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\right)\mathcal{P}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
