\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\right\|

(53)

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\right)\mathcal{P}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left|\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\right|\mathcal{P}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left|\hat{P}_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}\right|\mathcal{P}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}\right\|

(by mean-value theorem)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\max_{i\neq j}|\hat{P}_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}|\left\|\mathcal{P}\sum_{i\neq j}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{4e^{c_{P}}}\max_{i\neq j}|\hat{P}_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}|\left\|\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\right\|.

Note that

\displaystyle\max_{i\neq j}|\hat{P}_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{F}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty},

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n^{3/2}}c_{41},

where the last inequality follows from (
17
) and Assumption
4
.
Since we have
\|\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|\leq\overline{c}_{D^{*}}
, it then holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\right)\mathcal{P}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}(e_{i}+e_{j})\\
z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\cdot\left(\frac{c_{z}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n^{3/2}}c_{41}\right)\cdot\overline{c}_{D^{*}}.

(54)

For the second term, note that for any
\Delta
,

\displaystyle\left|{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\right){\sf vec}(\Delta)\right|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\Bigg(\frac{2}{n}\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle e_{i}^{T}\Delta_{X},e_{j}^{T}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})\rangle+\frac{2}{n}\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y},e_{i}^{T}(\hat{X}-X^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\frac{2}{n^{2}}\langle e^{T}_{j}\Delta_{Y},e^{T}_{i}\hat{X}\rangle\langle e_{i}^{T}\Delta_{X},e_{j}^{T}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})\rangle+\frac{2}{n^{2}}\langle e^{T}_{i}\Delta_{X},e^{T}_{j}Y^{*}\rangle\langle e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y},e_{i}^{T}(\hat{X}-X^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\langle e^{T}_{i}\Delta_{X},e^{T}_{j}\hat{Y}\rangle\langle e_{i}^{T}\Delta_{X},e_{j}^{T}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})\rangle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\langle e^{T}_{i}\Delta_{X},e^{T}_{j}Y^{*}\rangle\langle e_{i}^{T}\Delta_{X},e_{j}^{T}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\langle e^{T}_{j}\Delta_{Y},e^{T}_{i}\hat{X}\rangle\langle e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y},e_{i}^{T}(\hat{X}-X^{*})\rangle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\langle e^{T}_{j}\Delta_{Y},e^{T}_{i}X^{*}\rangle\langle e_{j}^{T}\Delta_{Y},e_{i}^{T}(\hat{X}-X^{*})\rangle\Bigg)\Bigg|.

Note that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle e_{i}^{T}\Delta_{X},e_{j}^{T}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}|\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle|^{2}}\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}|\langle e_{i}^{T}\Delta_{X},e_{j}^{T}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})\rangle|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\cdot c_{z}\|\Delta_{H}\|_{F}\cdot\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}\|\hat{Y}-Y^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(i)}\frac{c_{z}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\Delta_{H}\|_{F}\cdot\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}(\|\Delta_{H}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}),

where (i) follows from Lemma
C.1
.
Similar arguments hold for the other terms, for which we omit the proofs. We then have

\displaystyle\left|{\sf vec}(\Delta)^{T}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\right){\sf vec}(\Delta)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}c_{11}+\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}(\|\Delta_{H}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{c_{z}c_{11}+\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\sf vec}(\Delta)\|_{2}^{2}.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\left(\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}(e_{i}+e_{j})\\
z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}-\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}(e_{i}+e_{j})\\
z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}Y^{*}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}X^{*}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}c_{11}+\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}.

(55)

Combine (
53
), (
54
) and (
55
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\cdot\left(\frac{c_{z}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n^{3/2}}c_{41}\right)\cdot\overline{c}_{D^{*}}+\frac{c_{z}c_{11}+\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\left(c_{z}c_{11}\overline{c}_{D^{*}}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n}(c_{41}\overline{c}_{D^{*}}+c_{11})\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}.

By Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\lambda_{i}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})-\lambda_{i}(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})|\lesssim\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Since
\underline{c}_{D^{*}}\leq\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})\leq\lambda_{\max}(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})\leq\overline{c}_{D^{*}}
, we then have
\frac{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}{2}\leq\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})\leq\lambda_{\max}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})\leq{2\overline{c}_{D^{*}}}
as long as
n
is large enough.

∎

G.1
Proofs of Proposition
D.1

By (
19
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}^{d}-\hat{H}\\
\hat{X}^{d}-\hat{X}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})}\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\|_{F}.

Note that by (
18
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\lambda\hat{X}\\
\lambda\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\lesssim n^{-5},

which then gives

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq cn^{-5}+\lambda(\|\hat{X}\|_{F}+\|\hat{Y}\|_{F})

\displaystyle\leq cn^{-5}+\lambda(\|\hat{X}-X^{*}\|_{F}+\|\hat{Y}-Y^{*}\|_{F})+\lambda(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F})

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda\|X^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}

as long as
\|\hat{X}-X^{*}\|_{F}\ll\|X^{*}\|_{F}
and
n^{-5}\ll\lambda\|X^{*}\|_{F}
.
By Lemma
G.1
, we have
\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})\geq\underline{c}_{D^{*}}/2
.
As a result, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}^{d}-\hat{H}\\
\hat{X}^{d}-\hat{X}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{{\lambda}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}.

G.2
Proofs of Proposition
D.2

By (
21
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\nabla L({H^{*}},{X^{*}},{Y^{*}})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}{D^{*}}\mathcal{P})}\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L({H^{*}},{X^{*}},{Y^{*}})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}{D^{*}}\mathcal{P})}\|\nabla L({H^{*}},{X^{*}},{Y^{*}})\|_{F}.

By Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla L({H^{*}},{X^{*}},{Y^{*}})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim c_{z}\sqrt{p\log n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}(\|{X^{*}}\|_{F}+\|{Y^{*}}\|_{F})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}{n}}.

Note that
\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{P}{D^{*}}\mathcal{P})\geq\underline{c}_{D^{*}}
.
As a result, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{1}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}\log n}{n}}.

In order to show the second part of the result, we introduce the following lemma first.

Lemma G.2
.

Consider some fixed constants
a_{ij}
for
i\neq j\in[n]
, and random variable

\displaystyle X=\sum_{i\neq j}a_{ij}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}-A_{ij}\right).

Then with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
we have

\displaystyle|X|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\text{Var}[X]\cdot\log n}

Proof.

Denote by
X_{ij}=a_{ij}(e^{P_{ij}^{*}}/(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})-A_{ij})
. Then we know that
\mathbb{E}X_{ij}=0
and
|X_{ij}|\leq a_{ij}
. Therefore, by Hoeffding inequality, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i\neq j}X_{ij}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\log n\cdot\sum_{i\neq j}a_{ij}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(56)

On the other hand, since
A_{ij}
are independent random variables, we know that

\displaystyle\text{Var}[X]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}\text{Var}[X_{ij}]=\sum_{i\neq j}a_{ij}^{2}\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\geq\frac{e^{c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}a_{ij}^{2}.

As a result, we have
\sum_{i\neq j}a_{ij}^{2}\lesssim e^{-c_{P}}(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}\text{Var}[X]
. Combine this with (
56
) we get

\displaystyle|X|=\left|\sum_{i\neq j}X_{ij}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\text{Var}[X]\cdot\log n}

with probability exceeding
1-O(n^{-11})
.
∎

Let’s come back to control

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}.

According to the definition of
\bar{H},\bar{X},\bar{Y}
from (
21
), we know that each entry of
\bar{H}-{H^{*}},\bar{X}-{X^{*}},\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}
can be written as linear combinations of
e^{P_{ij}^{*}}/(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})-A_{ij}
, since
\nabla L(H^{*},X^{*},Y^{*})
is a linear combination of
e^{P_{ij}^{*}}/(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})-A_{ij}
. Then by Lemma
G.2
, we know that given any index
i
we have

\displaystyle\left|\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}_{i}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\text{Var}\left[\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}_{i}\right]\cdot\log n}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
. Taking a union bound for all indices
i
we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{e^{c_{P}}}\max_{i}\text{Var}\left[\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}_{i}\right]\cdot\log n}

(57)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
. On the other hand, from (
21
) we know that

\displaystyle\text{Var}\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-H^{*}\\
\bar{X}-X^{*}\\
\bar{Y}-Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}=(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}.

Therefore, one can see that

\displaystyle\max_{i}\text{Var}\left[\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-H^{*}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}_{i}\right]\leq\left\|\text{Var}\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-H^{*}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}\right\|=\left\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\right\|\leq\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{-1}.

Plugging this in (
57
) we get

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\bar{H}-{H^{*}}\\
\bar{X}-{X^{*}}\\
\bar{Y}-{Y^{*}}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}e^{c_{P}}}\cdot\log n}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.

G.3
Proofs of Theorem
D.3

We first prove the following lemma.

Lemma G.3
.

Under Assumption
6
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{F}^{d}-\bar{F}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c_{\infty}\sqrt{r},

where

\displaystyle c_{\infty}\asymp\frac{\lambda\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{c_{11}\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}+c_{2,\infty}c_{11}\sqrt{r^{2}+p}

and
\hat{c}
is defined in Lemma
G.1
.

Proof of Lemma
G.3
.

By (
19
) and (
21
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}^{d}-\bar{H}\\
\hat{X}^{d}-\bar{X}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-\bar{Y}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}-{H}^{*}\\
\hat{X}-{X}^{*}\\
\hat{Y}-{Y}^{*}\end{bmatrix}+\left((\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}-(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\right)\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla L({H}^{*},{X}^{*},{Y}^{*})-\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\right).

(58)

For notation simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle V^{*}:=\begin{bmatrix}{H}^{*}\\
{X}^{*}\\
{Y}^{*}\end{bmatrix}\text{ and }\hat{V}:=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}\\
\hat{X}\\
\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}.

We can further decompose the third term on the RHS of (
58
) as

\displaystyle(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla L({H}^{*},{X}^{*},{Y}^{*})-\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\left(\int^{1}_{0}\left(\nabla^{2}L(\hat{V}+t(V^{*}-\hat{V}))-\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})\right)dt(V^{*}-\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle+(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})-D^{*}\right)(V^{*}-\hat{V})+\left((\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})-I\right)(V^{*}-\hat{V})+(V^{*}-\hat{V}).

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{X}^{d}-\bar{X}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-\bar{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\|\left((\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}-(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\right)\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{V})\right\|_{2}}_{(1)}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\left(\int^{1}_{0}\left(\nabla^{2}L(\hat{V}+t(V^{*}-\hat{V}))-\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})\right)dt(V^{*}-\hat{V})\right)\right\|_{2}}_{(2)}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})-D^{*}\right)(V^{*}-\hat{V})\right\|_{2}}_{(3)}+\underbrace{\left\|\left((\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})-I\right)(V^{*}-\hat{V})\right\|_{\infty}}_{(4)}.

(59)

In the following, we bound (1)-(4), respectively.

1.

For (1), by Theorem 3.3 in
Stewart, (1977)
, we have

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}-(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}\max\{\|(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2},\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2}\}\cdot\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|.

By Lemma
G.1
, we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2},\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2}\}\lesssim\frac{1}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}},\quad\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}-(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}\sqrt{n}}.

Further, as shown in the proof of Proposition
D.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{V})\|_{F}\lesssim\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle(1)\leq\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}-(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{V})\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\lambda\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}.

2.

We then bound (2). Denote
V^{t}=\hat{V}+t(V^{*}-\hat{V})
and define
D^{t}
correspondingly. Following the same argument as in the proof of Lemma
G.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}D^{t}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Further, as already being shown in the proof of Lemma
B.1
,
we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}L(V^{t})-D^{t}\|\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\cdot\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|\hat{F}-F^{*}\|_{F}\right)\lesssim\frac{c_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})-D^{*}\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}.

Consequently, we have for all
t\in[0,1]

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla^{2}L(\hat{V}+t(V^{*}-\hat{V}))-\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})\right)\mathcal{P}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla^{2}L(V^{t})-D^{t}\|+\|\mathcal{P}D^{t}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|+\|\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})-D^{*}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle(2)\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|\|V^{*}-\hat{V}\|_{F}\leq\frac{c_{11}\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}.

3.

For (3), we have

\displaystyle(3)\leq\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|\|\nabla^{2}L(V^{*})-D^{*}\|\|V^{*}-\hat{V}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{1}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}\cdot c_{11}\sqrt{n}=\frac{c_{11}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}.

4.

Finally, for (4), we have

\displaystyle(4)\leq\left\|I-(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})\right\|_{2,\infty}\|V^{*}-\hat{V}\|_{F}\leq c_{2,\infty}c_{11}\sqrt{r^{2}+p},

where the last inequality follows from Assumption
6
.

Combine the bounds for (1)-(4), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{X}^{d}-\bar{X}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-\bar{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}\lesssim\frac{\lambda\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{c_{11}\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}+c_{2,\infty}c_{11}\sqrt{r^{2}+p}=c_{\infty}.

Consequently, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\hat{F}^{d}-\bar{F}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{r}\|\hat{F}^{d}-\bar{F}\|_{\infty}\lesssim c_{\infty}\sqrt{r}.

We then finish the proofs.
∎

With Lemma
G.3
in hand, we then prove Theorem
D.3
in the following.

Proof of Theorem
D.3
.

For notation simplicity, given
V=\begin{bmatrix}H\\
X\\
Y\end{bmatrix}
, we let
c(V):=\begin{bmatrix}H\\
XY^{\top}\end{bmatrix}
, which is the convex counterpart of
V
.
We denote

\displaystyle\Delta=\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}:=c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d}),\ \begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{X}\\
\Delta_{Y}\end{bmatrix}:=\begin{bmatrix}\bar{X}-\hat{X}^{d}\\
\bar{Y}-\hat{Y}^{d}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\Delta^{\prime}=\begin{bmatrix}\Delta^{\prime}_{H}\\
\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}:=c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(V^{*}),\ \begin{bmatrix}\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\\
\Delta^{\prime}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H}\\
\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}:=c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}),\ \begin{bmatrix}\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\\
\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\bar{X}-\hat{X}\\
\bar{Y}-\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}_{H}\\
\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}:=c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*}),\ \begin{bmatrix}\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\\
\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\bar{X}-X^{*}\\
\bar{Y}-Y^{*}\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{H}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}:=c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V}),\ \begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{X}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{X}^{d}-\hat{X}\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix},

which will be used in the following proofs.
By Proposition
D.1
, Proposition
D.2
and Lemma
C.1
, all the Frobenius norms related to
H,X,Y
(e.g.,
\|\Delta_{H}\|_{F}
,
\|\Delta_{X}\|_{F}
,
\|\Delta_{Y}\|_{F}
) are bounded by
(c_{a}+c_{11})\sqrt{n}
. Additionally, all the Frobenius norms related to
\Gamma
(e.g.,
\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|_{F}
) are bounded by
(c_{a}+c_{11})\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}n}
.

We define the quadratic approximation of the convex loss (defined in (
12
)) as

\displaystyle\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(V)\right):={L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)+\nabla{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(V)-c(V^{*})\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left(c(V)-c(V^{*})\right)^{\top}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(V)-c(V^{*})\right),

which implies for all
V

\displaystyle\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(V)\right)=\nabla{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)+\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(V)-c(V^{*})\right).

(60)

Note that

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}^{d}))

\displaystyle=\nabla L_{c}(c({V}^{*}))+\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))dt

\displaystyle=\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(\bar{V})\right)-\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))dt,

where the second equation follows from (
60
) with
V=\bar{V}
. Rearranging the terms gives

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int^{1}_{0}\left(\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(V^{*}))\right)dt(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))

\displaystyle-\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}^{d}))+\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(\bar{V})\right).

By multiplying both sides with
c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})
, we have

\displaystyle\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{\top}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{\top}\left(\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(V^{*}))dt\right)(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))}_{(1)}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}^{d}))^{\top}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)}_{(2)}+\underbrace{\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(\bar{V})\right)^{\top}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)}_{(3)}.

(61)

For the LHS of (
61
), we have

\displaystyle\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{\Gamma},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{e^{P_{ii}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ii}^{*}})^{2}}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{i}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{\Gamma},e_{i}e_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\sum_{i,j}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{\Gamma},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\langle\Delta_{H},z_{i}z_{i}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta_{\Gamma},e_{i}e_{i}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\left\|Z\Delta_{H}Z^{\top}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left((z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i})^{2}+\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{e^{c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\underline{c}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)-\frac{c_{z}^{2}}{2n}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}^{2}}{n^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}^{2}}{n^{2}}

(62)

as long as
n\geq(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}c_{z}^{2}/(\underline{c}e^{c_{P}})
. To control
\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}^{2}
, we write
\Delta_{\Gamma}=\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}-\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime}}
. Then
\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}^{2}\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}})_{ii}^{2}+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime}})_{ii}^{2}
. One can see that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime}})_{ii}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{d\top}-X^{*}Y^{*\top}\right)_{ii}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left((\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*})\hat{Y}^{d\top}+X^{*}(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*})^{\top}\right)_{ii}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left((\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*})_{i,\cdot}(\hat{Y}^{d})_{i,\cdot}^{\top}+(X^{*})_{i,\cdot}(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*})_{i,\cdot}^{\top}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\left\|\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*})_{i,\cdot}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|(\hat{Y}^{d})_{i,\cdot}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|(X^{*})_{i,\cdot}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*})_{i,\cdot}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\left\|\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*}\right\|_{F}^{2}\left\|\hat{Y}^{d}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}+\left\|X^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\left\|\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)

By Proposition
D.1
, Lemma
C.1
and Assumption
4
we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*}\right\|_{F},\left\|\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*}\right\|_{F}\lesssim c_{a}\sqrt{n}+c_{11}\sqrt{n},

\displaystyle\left\|X^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty},\left\|Y^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n}},

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{Y}^{d}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\left\|\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*}\right\|_{F}+\left\|Y^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n}}+c_{a}\sqrt{n}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime}})_{ii}^{2}\leq 4(c_{a}+c_{11})^{2}n\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n}}+c_{a}\sqrt{n}\right)^{2}.

Similarly, for
\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}})_{ii}^{2}
we also have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}})_{ii}^{2}\leq 4(c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{11})^{2}n\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n}}+c_{a}^{\prime}\sqrt{n}\right)^{2}.

Combine them together, we know that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}^{2}\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}})_{ii}^{2}+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{{}^{\prime}})_{ii}^{2}\leq 8((c_{a}+c_{11})^{2}+(c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{11})^{2})n\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n}}+(c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{a})\sqrt{n}\right)^{2}.

Plugging this back to (
62
), we know that

\displaystyle\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)-C_{0}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n^{2}}}+c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{a}\right)^{2},

(63)

where we define
C_{0}:=4((c_{a}+c_{11})^{2}+(c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{11})^{2})
.

For the RHS of (
61
), we will bound (1), (2) and (3), respectively.

1.

We first bound (1). We denote

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}H^{t}\\
\Gamma^{t}\end{bmatrix}:=c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*})),\ P_{ij}^{t}:=z_{i}^{T}H^{t}z_{j}+\frac{\Gamma^{t}_{ij}}{n}.

It then holds that

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(V^{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\right)\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
\frac{1}{n}{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\left(\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(V^{*}))\right)(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(\Delta^{\prime}_{H})\\
{\sf vec}(\frac{1}{n}\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma})\end{bmatrix}^{T}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\right)\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(\Delta_{H})\\
{\sf vec}(\frac{1}{n}\Delta_{\Gamma})\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(\Delta^{\prime}_{H})\\
{\sf vec}(\frac{1}{n}\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma})\end{bmatrix}^{T}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{c}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}_{c}\right)\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(\Delta_{H})\\
{\sf vec}(\frac{1}{n}\Delta_{\Gamma})\end{bmatrix}.

Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{c}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}_{c}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left|\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\right|\mathcal{P}_{c}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}_{c}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left|P_{ij}^{t}-P_{ij}^{*}\right|\mathcal{P}_{c}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\mathcal{P}_{c}\right\|

(mean-value theorem)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i\neq j}\left|P_{ij}^{t}-P_{ij}^{*}\right|\cdot\left\|\mathcal{P}_{c}\sum_{i,j}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{T}\mathcal{P}_{c}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\overline{c}\max_{i\neq j}\left|P_{ij}^{t}-P_{ij}^{*}\right|.

By the definition of
P^{t}_{ij}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\neq j}\left|P_{ij}^{t}-P_{ij}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\leq(\max_{i\neq j}\|z_{i}\|\|z_{j}\|)\cdot\|H^{t}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Gamma^{t}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|H^{t}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Gamma^{t}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|\hat{H}^{d}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\hat{\Gamma}^{d}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty},

where the last inequality follows from the definition of
H^{t},\Gamma^{t}
. Moreover, notice that

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Gamma}^{d}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\|(\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*})(\hat{Y}^{d})^{T}+X^{*}(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*})^{T}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{X}^{d}-X^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{Y}^{d}\|_{2,\infty}+\|X^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{Y}^{d}-Y^{*}\|_{2,\infty},

where
\|\hat{Y}^{d}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|\hat{Y}^{d}-\bar{Y}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\bar{Y}-{Y}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}+\|{Y}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 3\|{Y}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Gamma}^{d}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\leq 4\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{F}^{d}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

and

\displaystyle\max_{i\neq j}\left|P_{ij}^{t}-P_{ij}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|\hat{H}^{d}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{F}^{d}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}}{n}(\|\hat{H}^{d}-\hat{H}\|_{F}+\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F})+\frac{1}{n}\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}(\|\hat{F}^{d}-\bar{F}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\bar{F}-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{z}(c_{11}+c_{a})+\sqrt{\mu r^{2}\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}(c_{\infty}+c_{b})}{\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mu r^{2}\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}(c_{\infty}+c_{b})}{\sqrt{n}},

where the third inequality follows from Lemma
G.3
, Proposition
D.1
, Proposition
D.2
and Lemma
C.1
.
Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{t}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{t}})^{2}}-\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}\right)\mathcal{P}_{c}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\sf vec}(z_{i}z_{j}^{T})\\
{\sf vec}(e_{i}e_{j}^{T})\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\mathcal{P}_{c}\right\|\lesssim\frac{\overline{c}\sqrt{\mu r^{2}\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}(c_{\infty}+c_{b})}{\sqrt{n}}

and for all
t\in[0,1]

\displaystyle\left|\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{\top}\left(\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c({V}^{*})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(V^{*}))\right)(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*}))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\overline{c}\sqrt{\mu r^{2}\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}(c_{\infty}+c_{b})}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\Delta^{\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}.

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle|(1)|\lesssim\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\Delta^{\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}},

where
C_{1}:=\overline{c}\sqrt{\mu r^{2}\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}(c_{\infty}+c_{b})
.

2.

We then bound (2).
Note that

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}^{d}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))^{\top}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle+\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{\top}\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c(\hat{V})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V}))\right)dt\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right).

(64)

For the first term, recall the definition of
\Delta^{\prime\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V})\right)=\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right).

Note that

\displaystyle\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle=\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\hat{Y}^{T}+\hat{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle+\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

Thus we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}}_{Y},e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla L(\hat{V})^{T}(\bar{V}-\hat{V})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

(65)

By (
20
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{V})=-\mathcal{P}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)(\hat{V}^{d}-\hat{V}).

Moreover, note that
\mathcal{P}(\bar{V})=\bar{V},\mathcal{P}(\hat{V})=\hat{V},\mathcal{P}(\hat{V}^{d})=\hat{V}^{d}
.
Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\nabla L(\hat{V})^{T}(\bar{V}-\hat{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{V})\right)^{T}(\bar{V}-\hat{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\bar{V}-\hat{V})^{T}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\hat{Y}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}\hat{X}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)(\hat{V}^{d}-\hat{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\hat{Y}^{T}+\hat{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{Y}^{T}+\hat{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle-\frac{1}{n}\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle-\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

Combine the above result with (
65
), we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

(66)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))^{T}\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}|\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

(67)

Combine (
66
) and (
67
), we obtain

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)=-\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)+(r),

(68)

where

\displaystyle(r)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}|\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

By (
64
) and (
68
), we have

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(c(\hat{V}^{d}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle=\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\left(\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c(\hat{V})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))dt\right)\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)+(r).

(69)

As bounding (1), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\left(\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}L_{c}\left(c(\hat{V})+t(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V}))\right)-\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c(\hat{V}))dt\right)\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(\hat{V})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{C_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}

(70)

for
C_{2}=\overline{c}\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}/n^{2}}(\sqrt{r}(c_{\infty}+c_{b})+c_{43})
.
It remains to bound
(r)
.
Note that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y}\|_{F}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)^{2}}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\sqrt{\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}.

(by Assumption
3
)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\sqrt{\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\hat{\Delta}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\sqrt{\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}.

Also, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y}\|_{F}\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y}\|_{F}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left(\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}})^{2}}|\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle|^{2}\right|\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{2}.

Follow the proofs in Appendix
E
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|\hat{F}-F^{*}\|_{F}+{\sqrt{\log n}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}{1+e^{\hat{P}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\hat{\Delta}_{X}\hat{\Delta}^{T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|\hat{F}-F^{*}\|_{F}+{\sqrt{\log n}}\right).

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle|(r)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|X^{*}\|\|\hat{F}-F^{*}\|_{F}+{\sqrt{\log n}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3}\sqrt{n}.

Combine this with (
69
) and (
70
), we show that

\displaystyle|(2)|

\displaystyle=\left|L_{c}(c(\hat{V}^{d}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{C_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}+\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3}\sqrt{n}.

3.

We finally bound (3). By (
60
) with
V=\bar{V}
, we have

\displaystyle\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(\bar{V})\right)=\nabla{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)+\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*})\right),

which then implies

\displaystyle\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(\bar{V})\right)^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)+\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*})\right).

(71)

We will deal with the first term in the following. Recall the definition of
\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}
, follow the same argument as in (
65
), we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(V^{*}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*})\right)=\nabla L(V^{*})^{T}(\bar{V}-V^{*})+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

(72)

By (
22
), we have
\mathcal{P}\nabla L(V^{*})=-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\left(\bar{V}-V^{*}\right)
. Moreover, note that
\mathcal{P}(\bar{V})=\bar{V},\mathcal{P}(V^{*})=V^{*}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\nabla L(V^{*})^{T}(\bar{V}-V^{*})=\left(\mathcal{P}\nabla L(V^{*})\right)^{T}(\bar{V}-V^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(\bar{V}-V^{*}\right)^{T}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{P^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{P^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}{Y^{*}}\\
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}e_{i}^{T}{X^{*}}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)(\bar{V}-V^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(c(\bar{V})-c({V}^{*})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c({V^{*}}))\left(c(\bar{V})-c({V}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\left|\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|^{2},

where the last equation follows the same argument as that in bounding (2). Plug this into (
72
), we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(V^{*}))^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(c(\bar{V})-c({V}^{*})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c({V^{*}}))\left(c(\bar{V})-c({V}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\left|\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

(73)

Similarly, recall the definition of
\Delta^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(c(V^{*}))^{T}\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c(V^{*})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(c(\hat{V}^{d})-c({V}^{*})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(c({V^{*}}))\left(c(\bar{V})-c({V}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

(74)

Combine (
73
) and (
74
), we then have

\displaystyle\nabla{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)=-\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(V^{*})\right)+(r),

(75)

where

\displaystyle(r)=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\left|\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right|^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{dT}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\cdot\left(\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{H},z_{i}z_{j}^{T}\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\langle\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{T}-X^{*}Y^{*T},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}})^{2}}\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle\langle\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime\prime\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\frac{e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}{1+e^{{P}^{*}_{ij}}}-A_{ij}\right)\langle\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\Delta^{{}^{\prime}T}_{Y},e_{i}e_{j}^{T}\rangle.

By (
71
) and (
75
), we have

\displaystyle\nabla\bar{L}_{c}\left(c(\bar{V})\right)^{T}\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)=(r).

Following the same argument as in bounding (2), we have

\displaystyle|(3)|=

\displaystyle|(r)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}}{n}\left(\|\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{\|\Delta^{\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left(\|\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\left(\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\log n}}{n}\left(\|\Delta^{\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{X}\|_{F}^{2}+\|\Delta^{\prime\prime\prime}_{Y}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3}\sqrt{n}.

Combine the results for (1)-(3), by (
61
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)^{T}\nabla^{2}{L}_{c}\left(c(V^{*})\right)\left(c(\bar{V})-c(\hat{V}^{d})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\Delta^{\prime}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta^{\prime}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\hat{\Delta}_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}+\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3}\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C_{2}(c_{a}+c_{11})\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}+\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3}\sqrt{n}.

Further combine with (
63
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}e^{c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}\right)-C_{0}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n^{2}}}+c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{a}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C_{2}(c_{a}+c_{11})\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}+\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3}\sqrt{n},

which implies

\displaystyle\sqrt{\|\Delta_{H}\|^{2}_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}\|^{2}_{F}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{2(1+e^{c_{P}})^{2}}{\underline{c}e^{c_{P}}}}\left(\frac{\overline{c}\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}\right)^{1/4}(c_{a}+c_{11})^{3/2}\cdot n^{1/4}

as long as
n
is large enough to make
C_{0}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{max}}{n^{2}}}+c_{a}^{\prime}+c_{a}\right)^{2}
and
C_{2}(c_{a}+c_{11})\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}}
negligible terms compared to
\sqrt{n}
. Thus, we finish the proofs.
∎

Proofs of Proposition

In this example, we have

\displaystyle\Gamma^{*}=\begin{bmatrix}p\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}&q\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\\
q\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}&p\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\\
\end{bmatrix}=\Pi\begin{bmatrix}p&q\\
q&p\\
\end{bmatrix}\Pi^{T}=\Pi\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&-1\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{p+q}{2}&0\\
0&\frac{p-q}{2}\\
\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\
1&-1\\
\end{bmatrix}\Pi^{T},

where

with the first

and the last
n/2
rows being
[0,1]
. It then holds that

\displaystyle X^{*}=Y^{*}=\Pi\begin{bmatrix}1&1\\

\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}}&0\\
0&\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\\
\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}}\mathbf{1}&\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\mathbf{1}\\
\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}}\mathbf{1}&-\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\mathbf{1}\end{bmatrix},

(76)

where
\mathbf{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{\frac{n}{2}\times 1}
.
We denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}:=\{(i,j)\mid 1\leq i,j\leq n/2,i\neq j\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{2}:=\{(i,j)\mid 1\leq i\leq n/2,n/2<j\leq n,i\neq j\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{3}:=\{(i,j)\mid n/2<i\leq n,1\leq j\leq n/2,i\neq j\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{4}:=\{(i,j)\mid n/2<i,j\leq n,i\neq j\}.

Since
H^{*}=0
, we then have

\displaystyle D^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(i,j)\in\mathcal{A}_{1}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\\[10.0pt]
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}+\sum_{(i,j)\in\mathcal{A}_{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},-\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\\[10.0pt]
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{(i,j)\in\mathcal{A}_{3}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\\[10.0pt]
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},-\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}+\sum_{(i,j)\in\mathcal{A}_{4}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\frac{1}{n}e_{i}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},-\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\\[10.0pt]
\frac{1}{n}e_{j}\left[\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},-\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}\right]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}.

In what follows, we view
D^{*}
as a

matrix and denote

\displaystyle D^{*}=\begin{bmatrix}D_{11}^{*}&D_{12}^{*}&D_{13}^{*}&D_{14}^{*}\\
D_{21}^{*}&D_{22}^{*}&D_{23}^{*}&D_{24}^{*}\\
D_{31}^{*}&D_{32}^{*}&D_{33}^{*}&D_{34}^{*}\\
D_{41}^{*}&D_{42}^{*}&D_{43}^{*}&D_{44}^{*}\end{bmatrix},

where

.
Recall that we have

\displaystyle D^{*}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{X}\\
\Delta_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}X^{*}\\
Y^{*}\end{bmatrix},

where we view
\Delta_{X},\Delta_{Y},X^{*},Y^{*}
as

vectors. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}D_{11}^{*}&D_{12}^{*}\\
D_{21}^{*}&D_{22}^{*}\end{bmatrix}\Delta_{X}+\begin{bmatrix}D_{13}^{*}&D_{14}^{*}\\
D_{23}^{*}&D_{24}^{*}\\
\end{bmatrix}\Delta_{Y}=X^{*}.

(77)

Based on the specific form of
X^{*}
and
Y^{*}
, it can be seen that the solutions must have the following form:
Why???

\displaystyle\Delta_{X}=\Delta_{Y}={\sf vec}\begin{bmatrix}a&b\\
\vdots&\vdots\\
a&b\\
c&d\\
\vdots&\vdots\\
c&d\\
\end{bmatrix}.

(78)

The matrix on the RHS has its first

[a,b]
and last
n

[c,d]
for some
a,b,c,d
.
By (

), we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}D_{11}^{*}+D_{13}^{*}&D_{12}^{*}+D_{14}^{*}\\
D_{21}^{*}+D_{23}^{*}&D_{22}^{*}+D_{24}^{*}\end{bmatrix}\Delta_{X}=X^{*}.

(79)

With a little abuse of notion, we denote
e_{i},e_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{\frac{n}{2}\times 1}
the one-hot vector in the following. And we denote
s=\sqrt{\frac{p+q}{2}},t=\sqrt{\frac{p-q}{2}}
. It then holds that

\displaystyle D_{11}^{*}+D_{13}^{*}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}\end{subarray}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}\end{subarray}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes}\begin{bmatrix}e_{j}[s,t]\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,-t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle D_{12}^{*}+D_{14}^{*}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,-t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes}\begin{bmatrix}e_{j}[s,t]\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle D_{21}^{*}+D_{23}^{*}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes}\begin{bmatrix}e_{j}[s,-t]\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle D_{22}^{*}+D_{24}^{*}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}\end{subarray}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,-t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j\\
1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}\end{subarray}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,-t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes}\begin{bmatrix}e_{j}[s,-t]\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq\frac{n}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}e_{i}[s,t]\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}.

(80)

Combine (
76
), (
78
), (
79
), (
80
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,t]=(n-2)\frac{as+bt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,t])+\frac{n}{2}\frac{as-bt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,-t])

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{n}{2}\frac{cs+dt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,-t])

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,-t]=(n-2)\frac{cs-dt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,-t])+\frac{n}{2}\frac{cs+dt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,t])

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{n}{2}\frac{as-bt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{\frac{n}{2}}\cdot[s,t]),

which implies

\displaystyle\begin{cases}as-bt=-(cs+dt)\\
(n-2)\frac{as+bt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}=1\\
(n-2)\frac{cs-dt}{n^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}=1\end{cases}.

We denote

\displaystyle N:=\frac{n^{2}}{n-2}\frac{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}.

It then holds that

\displaystyle a=c=\frac{N}{2s},\quad b=-d=\frac{N}{2t}

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{X}=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{N}{2s}\mathbf{1}&\frac{N}{2t}\mathbf{1}\\[10.0pt]
\frac{N}{2s}\mathbf{1}&-\frac{N}{2t}\mathbf{1}\end{bmatrix}.

(81)

Combine with (
76
), we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{X}Y^{*\top}+X^{*}\Delta^{\top}_{Y}=\Delta_{X}X^{*\top}+X^{*}\Delta^{\top}_{X}=\begin{bmatrix}2N\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}&0\\
0&2N\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}.

By the definition of
M^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle M^{*}=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}&\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\\
\frac{e^{\frac{q}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{q}{n}})^{2}}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}&\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}&&\\
&\ddots&\\
&&\frac{e^{\frac{p}{n}}}{(1+e^{\frac{p}{n}})^{2}}\end{bmatrix}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot\left(\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{*\top}+X^{*}\Delta^{\top}_{Y})\right)

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{2}{n-2}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}&0\\
0&\frac{2}{n-2}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\frac{2}{n-2}&&\\
&\ddots&\\
&&\frac{2}{n-2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n-2}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}-I_{\frac{n}{2}}&0\\
0&\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}-I_{\frac{n}{2}}\end{bmatrix}.

Note that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}-I_{\frac{n}{2}}&0\\
0&\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}-I_{\frac{n}{2}}\end{bmatrix}

has
2
singular values equal to
\frac{n}{2}-1
and
n-2
singular values equal to
1
. And in this example, we have
r=2
. Thus we verify

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}\left(\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot\left(\frac{1}{n}(\Delta_{X}Y^{*\top}+X^{*}\Delta^{\top}_{Y})\right)\right)=\frac{2}{n-2}<\frac{1}{2}.

That is to say Assumption
7
holds with
{\epsilon}=1/2
. Thus, we finish the proofs.

H.2
Bridge convex optimizer and approximate nonconvex optimizer

In this section, we aim to prove the following theorem.

Theorem H.1
.

Suppose Assumption
7
holds. We then have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}_{c}-\hat{H}\\
\frac{1}{n}(\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{(1+e^{cc_{P}})^{2}}{\min\{\underline{c}/3,1/6\}e^{cc_{P}}}\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}

for some constant
c
.

H.2.1
Useful claims and lemmas

In this section, we establish several useful claims and lemmas that will support the proof of Theorem
H.1
.
For notation simplicity, in this section, we denote the solution given by gradient descent as
(H,X,Y)
instead of
(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})
.

Claim H.2
.

Let
U\Sigma V^{\top}
be the SVD of
XY^{\top}
. There exists an invertible matrix
Q\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
such that
X=U\Sigma^{1/2}Q
,
Y=V\Sigma^{1/2}Q^{-T}
and

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma_{Q}-\Sigma_{Q}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{8\sqrt{\kappa}}{\lambda\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}.

(82)

Here
U_{Q}\Sigma_{Q}V_{Q}^{\top}
is the SVD of Q.

Proof of Claim
H.2
.

Let

\displaystyle B_{1}:=\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})Y+\lambda X\quad\text{and}\quad B_{2}:=\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})^{\top}X+\lambda Y.

By the definition of
\nabla_{X}f(H,X,Y)
and
\nabla_{Y}f(H,X,Y)
, we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|{B}_{1}\|_{F},\|{B}_{2}\|_{F}\}

\displaystyle=\max\{\|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\nabla_{X}f(H,X,Y)\|_{F},\|\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\nabla_{Y}f(H,X,Y)\|_{F}\}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}.

In addition, the definition of
B_{1}
and
B_{2}
allow us to obtain

\displaystyle\|X^{\top}X-Y^{\top}Y\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda}\|X^{\top}({B}_{1}-\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})Y)-({B}_{2}-\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})^{\top}X)^{\top}Y\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda}\|X^{\top}{B}_{1}-{B}_{2}^{\top}Y\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\|X\|\|{B}_{1}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\|{B}_{2}\|_{F}\|Y\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}.

Here, the last inequality follows from the fact that
\|X\|,\|Y\|\leq 2\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}
. In view of Lemma
J.6
, one can find an invertible
{Q}
such that
X=U\Sigma^{1/2}{Q}
,
Y=V\Sigma^{1/2}{Q}^{-T}
and

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{Q}-\Sigma_{Q}^{-1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma)}\|X^{\top}X-Y^{\top}Y\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}\cdot\frac{4}{\lambda}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\sqrt{\kappa}}{\lambda\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F},

where
\Sigma_{Q}
is a diagonal matrix consisting of all singular values of
{Q}
. Here the second inequality follows from

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{\min}(XY^{\top})-\sigma_{\min}(X^{*}Y^{*\top})\right|\leq\left\|XY^{\top}-X^{*}Y^{*\top}\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\|X-X^{*}\|\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n},

which implies
\sigma_{\min}(XY^{\top})\geq\sigma_{\min}/2
as long as
c_{11}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}\ll\sigma_{\min}
.
This completes the proof.
∎

With Claim
H.2
in hand, we are ready to establish the following claim.

Claim H.3
.

Suppose that Assumption
7
holds. Under the same notations in Claim
H.2
, let
\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}=-\lambda UV^{\top}+R
.
Then
R
satisfies:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\leq\frac{72\kappa}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}\quad\text{and}\quad\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R)\|<\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\right)\lambda

for
{\epsilon}>0
specified in Assumption
7
. Here
\mathcal{T}
is the tangent space for
U\Sigma V^{\top}
defined as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}:=\{UA^{\top}+BV^{\top}\mid A,B\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}\},

\mathcal{T}^{\perp}
is the orthogonal complement of
\mathcal{T}
, and
\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}
,
\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}
are the orthogonal projection onto the subspace
\mathcal{T}
,
\mathcal{T}^{\perp}
, respectively.

Proof of Claim
H.3
.

Recall the notations in the proof of Claim
H.2
, we have

\displaystyle B_{1}=\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})Y+\lambda X\quad\text{and}\quad B_{2}=\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})^{\top}X+\lambda Y.

(83)

By the definition of
R
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}=R-\lambda UV^{\top}.

(84)

From the definition of
\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|UU^{\top}{R}(I-VV^{\top})+RVV^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|U^{\top}{R}(I-VV^{\top})\|_{F}+\|RVV^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|U^{\top}{R}\|_{F}+\|RV\|_{F}.

(85)

In addition, by Claim
H.2
, we have

\displaystyle X

\displaystyle=U\Sigma^{1/2}{Q}\quad\text{and}\quad Y=V\Sigma^{1/2}{Q}^{-T}

(86)

for some invertible matrix
{Q}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
, whose SVD
U_{Q}\Sigma_{Q}V_{Q}^{\top}
obeys (
82
). Combine (
83
) and (
84
), we have

\displaystyle-\lambda UV^{\top}Y+RY

\displaystyle=-\lambda X+{B}_{1},

which together with (
86
) yields

\displaystyle RV

\displaystyle=\lambda U\Sigma^{1/2}(I_{r}-{Q}{Q}^{\top})\Sigma^{-1/2}+{B}_{1}{Q}^{\top}\Sigma^{-1/2}.

Apply the triangle inequality to get

\displaystyle\|RV\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\lambda U\Sigma^{1/2}(I_{r}-{Q}{Q}^{\top})\Sigma^{-1/2}\|_{F}+\|{B}_{1}{Q}^{\top}\Sigma^{-1/2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\|\Sigma^{1/2}\|\|\Sigma^{-1/2}\|\|{Q}{Q}^{\top}-I_{r}\|_{F}+\|{Q}\|\|\Sigma^{-1/2}\|\|{B}_{1}\|_{F}.

(87)

In order to further upper bound (
87
), we first recognize the fact that as long as
c_{11}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}\ll\sigma_{\min}
,

\displaystyle\|\Sigma^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\sigma_{\max}},\quad\text{and}\quad\|\Sigma^{-1/2}\|=1/\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma)}\leq\sqrt{2/\sigma_{\min}},

which holds since

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{i}(XY^{\top})-\sigma_{i}(X^{*}Y^{*\top})\right|\leq\left\|XY^{\top}-X^{*}Y^{*\top}\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\|X-X^{*}\|\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}.

Second, Claim
H.2
and (
18
) yields

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{Q}-\Sigma_{Q}^{-1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\sqrt{\kappa}}{\lambda\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}\ll 1.

This in turn implies that
\|{Q}\|=\|\Sigma_{Q}\|\leq 2
. Putting the above bounds together yields

\displaystyle\|RV\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\sqrt{2\sigma_{\max}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\Sigma_{Q}^{2}-I_{r}\|_{F}+2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\sqrt{2\sigma_{\max}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\Sigma_{Q}\|\|\Sigma_{Q}-\Sigma_{Q}^{-1}\|_{F}+2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda\sqrt{2\sigma_{\max}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}}\frac{8\sqrt{\kappa}}{\lambda\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}+2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{36\kappa}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}.

Similarly, we can show that

\displaystyle\|U^{\top}{R}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{36\kappa}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}.

These bounds together with (
85
) result in

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{72\kappa}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}.

(88)

In the following, we establish the bound for
\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R)\|
. Note that

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}=-\lambda UV^{\top}+R=-\lambda UV^{\top}+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}({R}).

Suppose for the moment that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})\|_{F}\leq\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\lambda\quad\text{and}\quad\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)<\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{2}\right)\lambda.

(89)

Then, by Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(-\lambda UV^{\top}+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}({R})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(-\lambda UV^{\top}+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})+\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}({R})\right)+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})\|_{F}

\displaystyle<\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{2}\right)\lambda+\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\lambda

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\right)\lambda.

Since
-\lambda UV^{\top}\in\mathcal{T}
with
r
singular values of
\lambda
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R)\|<\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\right)\lambda.

Thus, it remains to verify the two conditions listed in (
89
). For the first condition, by (
88
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}({R})\|_{F}\leq\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\lambda\quad\text{as long as}\quad\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(H,X,Y))\|_{F}\leq\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{288\kappa}\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}},

which is guaranteed by (
18
).
We then verify the second condition in the following. For notation simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle\ell_{ij}(x):=\log(1+e^{x})-A_{ij}x.

Then we have

\displaystyle\left(\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})-\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},{\Gamma}^{{*}})\right)_{ij}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\int^{1}_{0}\ell^{\prime\prime}_{ij}\left(P^{*}_{ij}+\tau(P_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij})\right)d\tau\cdot\frac{P_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}}{n}&i\neq j\\
0&i=j\end{cases}.

We define a matrix
H\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
such that

\displaystyle H_{ij}=\begin{cases}\int^{1}_{0}\ell^{\prime\prime}_{ij}\left(P^{*}_{ij}+\tau(P_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij})\right)d\tau&i\neq j\\
0&i=j\end{cases}.

It then holds that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},{\Gamma}^{{*}})+\frac{1}{n}H\odot(P-P^{*})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},{\Gamma}^{{*}})+\frac{1}{n}(H-M^{*})\odot(P-P^{*})+\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(P-P^{*}).

(90)

Recall the definition of
