M^{*}
where

\displaystyle M^{*}_{ij}=\begin{cases}\frac{e^{P_{ij}^{*}}}{(1+e^{P_{ij}^{*}})^{2}}&i\neq j\\
0&i=j\end{cases}=\begin{cases}\ell^{\prime\prime}_{ij}(P^{*}_{ij})&i\neq j\\
0&i=j\end{cases}.

We then have for
i\neq j
:

\displaystyle|M^{*}_{ij}-H_{ij}|\leq\int^{1}_{0}\left|\ell^{\prime\prime}_{ij}\left(P^{*}_{ij}+\tau(P_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij})\right)-\ell^{\prime\prime}_{ij}(P^{*}_{ij})\right|d\tau\leq\frac{1}{4}|P_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij}|,

where the last inequality follows from the mean-value theorem and the fact that
|\ell^{\prime\prime\prime}_{ij}(x)|\leq\ell^{\prime\prime}_{ij}(x)\leq 1/4
for any
x
.
This further implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}(H-M^{*})\odot(P-P^{*})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{n}(H-M^{*})\odot(P-P^{*})\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\cdot n\|P-P^{*}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|H-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|XY^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|H-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|F-F^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(c_{z}^{2}c_{11}^{2}+\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}c_{41}^{2}\right),

where the last inequality follows from (
17
) and Assumption
4
.
Thus, as long as
n
large enough, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}(H-M^{*})\odot(P-P^{*})\right\|\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(c_{z}^{2}c_{11}^{2}+\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n^{2}}c_{41}^{2}\right)<\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}.

(91)

Moreover, as shown in the proofs of Lemma
F.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})\right\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}<\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}

(92)

as long as
{\epsilon}\lambda>\sqrt{\frac{\log n}{n}}
.
Thus, it remains to deal with
\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(P-P^{*})
.
Note that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(P-P^{*})=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(\hat{P}^{d}-\bar{P})}_{(1)}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(\bar{P}-P^{*})}_{(2)}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(P-\hat{P}^{d})}_{(3)}.

(93)

In what follows, we will deal with (1),(2) and (3), respectively.

1.

For (1), note that

\displaystyle\|(1)\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(\hat{P}^{d}-\bar{P})\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}(\hat{P}^{d}_{ij}-\bar{P}_{ij})^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|\hat{H}^{d}-\bar{H}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\hat{X}^{d}\hat{Y}^{d\top}-\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{\top}\|_{F}\right),

where the last inequality follows the same trick we used before and we omit the details here. By Theorem
D.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|(1)\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\overline{c}}c_{d}}{n^{3/4}}<\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}

(94)

as long as
n
is large enough.

2.

For (2), note that

\displaystyle\|(2)\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{n}M^{*}\odot(\bar{P}-P^{*})\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sqrt{\sum_{i\neq j}(\bar{P}_{ij}-P^{*}_{ij})^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\left(\sqrt{\overline{c}}\|\bar{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\bar{X}\bar{Y}^{\top}-X^{*}Y^{*\top}\|_{F}\right).

By Proposition
D.2
, we have

\displaystyle\|(2)\|\lesssim c^{\prime}_{a}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}{n^{3/2}}\right)<\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}

(95)

as long as
{\epsilon}\lambda>c^{\prime}_{a}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\overline{c}}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}}{n^{3/2}}\right)
.

3.

We denote
\hat{\Delta}
such that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{H}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{X}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}^{d}-H\\
\hat{X}^{d}-X\\
\hat{Y}^{d}-Y\end{bmatrix}.

With a little abuse of notation, we denote
(\Delta_{H},\Delta_{X},\Delta_{Y})
such that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{X}\\
\Delta_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\lambda X^{*}\\
\lambda Y^{*}\end{bmatrix},

Then by Assumption
7
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left({z}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})_{ij}}{n}\right)_{ij}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)<(1-\epsilon)\lambda

for some
{\epsilon}>0
.
We further have the following claim.

Claim H.4
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{X}-{\Delta}_{X}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{Y}-{\Delta}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\leq c_{\text{diff}},

where

\displaystyle c_{\text{diff}}\asymp\lambda\left(\frac{\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{c_{11}\sqrt{n}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}\right).

(96)

Proof of Claim
H.4
.

By the definition of
(\Delta_{H},\Delta_{X},\Delta_{Y})
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{X}\\
\Delta_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=-(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\begin{bmatrix}0\\
-\lambda X^{*}\\
-\lambda Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}.

By the definition of the debiased estimator, we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{H}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{X}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}=-(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\left(\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\right),

For notation simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle A_{1}:=(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger},\ b_{1}:=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
-\lambda X^{*}\\
-\lambda Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}

and

\displaystyle A_{2}:=(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger},\ b_{2}:=\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}).

It then holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{X}-{\Delta}_{X}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{Y}-{\Delta}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|A_{1}b_{1}-A_{2}b_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|\|b_{2}\|_{F}+\|A_{1}\|\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}.

(97)

In the following, we control
\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|
,
\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}
,
\|A_{1}\|
, and
\|b_{2}\|_{F}
, respectively.

(a)

For
\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|
, by Theorem 3.3 in
Stewart, (1977)
, we have

\displaystyle\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}\max\{\|(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2},\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2}\}\cdot\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|.

(98)

By Lemma
G.1
, we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|(\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2},\|(\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P})^{\dagger}\|^{2}\}\lesssim\frac{1}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}},\quad\|\mathcal{P}\hat{D}\mathcal{P}-\mathcal{P}D^{*}\mathcal{P}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{n}}.

Finally, by (
98
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}\sqrt{n}}.

(99)

(b)

For
\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}
, we have

\displaystyle\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}0\\
-\lambda X^{*}\\
-\lambda Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}-\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\lambda X^{*}\\
\lambda Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\lambda\hat{X}\\
\lambda\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})-\begin{bmatrix}0\\
-\lambda\hat{X}\\
-\lambda\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

(100)

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\lambda X^{*}\\
\lambda Y^{*}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\lambda\hat{X}\\
\lambda\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}\leq\lambda(\|X^{*}-\hat{X}\|_{F}+(\|Y^{*}-\hat{Y}\|_{F})\leq 2\lambda c_{11}\sqrt{n},

(101)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
.
For the second term, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{P}\nabla L(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})-\begin{bmatrix}0\\
-\lambda\hat{X}\\
-\lambda\hat{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}=\|\mathcal{P}\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y})\|_{F}\lesssim n^{-5}.

(102)

Combine (
100
), (
101
) and (
102
), we have

\displaystyle\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}\lesssim\lambda c_{11}\sqrt{n}.

(c)

For
\|A_{1}\|
, by Lemma
G.1
, we have
\|A_{1}\|\leq 1/\underline{c}_{D^{*}}
.

(d)

For
\|b_{2}\|_{F}
, we have

\displaystyle\|b_{2}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}+\|b_{1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|b_{1}-b_{2}\|_{F}+\lambda(\|X^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F})

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}

as long as
\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}\gg c_{11}\sqrt{n}
.

Combine the above results with (
97
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{X}-{\Delta}_{X}\\
\hat{\Delta}_{Y}-{\Delta}_{Y}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}\sqrt{n}}\cdot\lambda\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}+\frac{1}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}\cdot\lambda c_{11}\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle=\lambda\left(\frac{\hat{c}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}+\frac{c_{11}\sqrt{n}}{\underline{c}_{D^{*}}}\right).

We then finish the proofs.

∎

By Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left((\hat{P}^{d}-P)-\frac{1}{n}(\hat{X}^{d}-X)(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y)^{\top}\right)\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left({z}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\Delta}_{H}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\hat{\Delta}_{X}{Y}^{T}+{X}{\hat{\Delta}^{\top}_{Y}})_{ij}}{n}\right)_{ij}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left({z}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})_{ij}}{n}\right)_{ij}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\left\|{M}^{*}\odot\left({z}_{i}^{\top}(\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}){z}_{j}\right)_{ij}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{M}^{*}\odot\left((\hat{\Delta}_{X}{Y}^{T}+{X}{\hat{\Delta}^{\top}_{Y}})-(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left({z}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})_{ij}}{n}\right)_{ij}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{4n}\left\|\left({z}_{i}^{\top}(\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}){z}_{j}\right)_{ij}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\left\|(\hat{\Delta}_{X}{Y}^{T}+{X}{\hat{\Delta}^{\top}_{Y}})-(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})\right\|_{F}.

By Claim
H.4
, we can bound the Frobenius norms on the RHS respectively.

(a)

For the first Frobenius norm, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left({z}_{i}^{\top}(\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}){z}_{j}\right)_{ij}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{i,j}\left({z}_{i}^{\top}(\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}){z}_{j}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}\|\sqrt{\sum_{i,j}\|z_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\|z_{j}\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{z}\|\hat{\Delta}_{H}-{\Delta}_{H}\|

\displaystyle\leq c_{z}c_{\text{diff}}.

(b)

For the second Frobenius norm, we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\hat{\Delta}_{X}{Y}^{T}+{X}{\hat{\Delta}^{\top}_{Y}})-(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}{Y}^{T}-\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}\|_{F}+\|{X}{\hat{\Delta}^{\top}_{Y}}-{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}\|Y-Y^{*}\|_{F}+\|Y^{*}\|_{F}\|\hat{\Delta}_{X}-{\Delta}_{X}\|_{F}+\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}\|X-X^{*}\|_{F}+\|X^{*}\|_{F}\|\hat{\Delta}_{Y}-{\Delta}_{Y}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim c_{a}c_{11}n+\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}c_{\text{diff}},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
, Proposition
D.1
, Claim
H.4
, and Assumption
4
.

Combine the above bounds, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left((\hat{P}^{d}-P)-\frac{1}{n}(\hat{X}^{d}-X)(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y)^{\top}\right)\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left({z}_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\Delta_{X}{Y}^{*T}+{X}^{*}{\Delta^{\top}_{Y}})_{ij}}{n}\right)_{ij}\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c}{n}\left(c_{a}c_{11}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n}c_{\text{diff}}+c_{z}c_{\text{diff}}\right)

\displaystyle<(1-{\epsilon})\lambda+\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{20}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{19{\epsilon}}{20}\right)\lambda

as long as
c_{a}c_{11}+\frac{\sqrt{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}}{n}c_{\text{diff}}+c_{z}c_{\text{diff}}\ll{\epsilon}\lambda n
(
n
is large enough).
By Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot(\hat{P}^{d}-P)\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left((\hat{P}^{d}-P)-\frac{1}{n}(\hat{X}^{d}-X)(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y)^{\top}\right)\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{M}^{*}\odot(\hat{X}^{d}-X)(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y)^{\top}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left((\hat{P}^{d}-P)-\frac{1}{n}(\hat{X}^{d}-X)(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y)^{\top}\right)\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)+\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\|\hat{X}^{d}-X\|_{F}\|\hat{Y}^{d}-Y\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot\left((\hat{P}^{d}-P)-\frac{1}{n}(\hat{X}^{d}-X)(\hat{Y}^{d}-Y)^{\top}\right)\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)+\frac{c_{a}^{2}}{4n}

(by Proposition
D.1
)

\displaystyle<\left(1-\frac{19{\epsilon}}{20}\right)\lambda+\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{20}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{9{\epsilon}}{10}\right)\lambda

(103)

as long as
c_{a}^{2}\ll{\epsilon}\lambda n
(
n
is large enough).

Combine (
93
), (
94
), (
95
), (
103
) and apply Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot(P-P^{*})\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot(P-\hat{P}^{d})\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)+\|(1)\|+\|(2)\|

\displaystyle<\left(1-\frac{9{\epsilon}}{10}\right)\lambda+\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}+\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{7{\epsilon}}{10}\right)\lambda.

(104)

Finally, combine (
90
), (
91
), (
92
), (
104
) and apply Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H,XY^{\top})\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{r+1}\left(\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\left(\frac{1}{n}{M}^{*}\odot(P-P^{*})\right)\mathcal{P}_{Z}^{\perp}\right)+\left\|\frac{1}{n}(H-M^{*})\odot(P-P^{*})\right\|+\left\|\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(H^{*},\Gamma^{*})\right\|

\displaystyle<\left(1-\frac{7{\epsilon}}{10}\right)\lambda+\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}+\frac{{\epsilon}\lambda}{10}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{2}\right)\lambda.

Thus, we finish the proof of Claim
H.3
.

∎

Lemma H.5
.

Given any
\Delta_{H}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
and
\Delta_{\Gamma}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
which satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{Z}\Delta_{\Gamma}=\Delta_{\Gamma}\mathcal{P}_{Z}=0,

then we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{j}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{j}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}\geq\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{j}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{j}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{1\leq i,j\leq n}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{j}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{j}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{i}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{i}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\left\|Z\Delta_{H}Z^{\top}+\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{j}+\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq_{(i)}\left\|Z\Delta_{H}Z^{\top}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(z_{i}^{\top}\Delta_{H}z_{i}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\underline{c}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|z_{i}\right\|_{2}^{4}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left\|\Delta_{H}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2},

as long as
n\geq 2c_{z}^{2}/\underline{c}
. Here (i) follows from the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{Z}(\Delta_{\Gamma})=0
,
\mathcal{P}_{Z}(Z\Delta_{H}Z^{\top})=Z\Delta_{H}Z^{\top}
, and
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
.
∎

H.2.2
Proof of Theorem
H.1

With Claim
H.3
and Lemma
H.5
in hand, we are ready to prove Theorem
H.1
in the following.

Proof of Theorem
H.1
.

In the following, we fix a constant
c=c_{P}
. We define a constraint convex optimization problem as

\displaystyle(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con}):={\arg\min}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathcal{P}_{Z}\Gamma=0,\hskip 8.19447pt\Gamma\mathcal{P}_{Z}=0,\\
\|{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}\leq cn,\ \|{\Gamma}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\leq cn\end{subarray}}f_{c}(H,\Gamma),

(105)

where
f_{c}
is the convex objective defined in (
13
).
By (
17
),
(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})
is feasible for the constraint of (
105
).
By the optimality of
(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})
, we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})+\lambda\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}\leq L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})+\lambda\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}.

(106)

We denote

\displaystyle\Delta_{H}^{con}:=\hat{H}^{con}-\hat{H},\ \Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}:=\hat{\Gamma}^{con}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}.

By mean value theorem, there exists a set of parameter
(\tilde{H},\tilde{\Gamma})
which is a convex combination of
(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})
and
(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})
such that

\displaystyle L_{c}(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})-L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\nabla^{2}L_{c}(\tilde{H},\tilde{\Gamma})\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{1}{2}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\tilde{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\tilde{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{j}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{j}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}.

(107)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})-L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\geq\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}.

Combine this with (
106
) we get

\displaystyle 0\leq-\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}+\lambda\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}-\lambda\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}.

(108)

In addition, by the convexity of
\|\cdot\|_{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}-\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}=\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}+\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}-\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}\geq\langle UV^{\top}+W,\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle

for any
W\in\mathcal{T}^{\perp}
obeying
\|W\|\leq 1
. In the following, we pick
W
such that
\langle W,\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle=\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}
. We then obtain
\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}-\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}\geq\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}
, and consequently, by (
108
), we have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq-\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}-\lambda\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle=-\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\end{bmatrix}-\lambda\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}-(\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z})^{\top}\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}-\lambda\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}.

Recall the definition of
R
in (
84
), we further have

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}-\langle R,\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}.

By Claim
H.3
, we have

\displaystyle-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R)\|\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}\leq\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\right)\lambda\cdot\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

As a result, one can see that

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{4}\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\|_{F}\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c^{\prime}}{n^{5}}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{72\kappa}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}\right)

(109)

with some constant
c^{\prime}>0
.

On the other hand, note that for some constant
c^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{e^{\tilde{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\tilde{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{j}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{j}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\geq_{(1)}\frac{e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}})^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\begin{bmatrix}{z}_{i}{z}_{j}^{\top}\\
\frac{{e}_{i}{e}_{j}^{\top}}{n}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}})^{2}}\left(\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}\right),

(110)

where the last inequality follows Lemma
H.5
.
Here (1) follows from the following argument: note that

\displaystyle|\hat{P}_{ij}|:=\left|{z}_{i}^{\top}\hat{H}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})_{ij}}{n}\right|\leq|P_{ij}^{*}|+\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}.

Further, by (
17
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\hat{X}-X^{*})\hat{Y}^{\top}+X^{*}(\hat{Y}-Y^{*})^{\top}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\hat{X}-X^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{Y}\|_{2,\infty}+\|X^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{Y}-Y^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

(Cauchy)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c_{41}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}.

Thus, by (
17
) and Assumption
2
, as long as
n
is large enough, we have
|\hat{P}_{ij}|\leq 2c_{P}
.
Similarly, we have

\displaystyle|(\hat{P}^{con})_{ij}|:=\left|{z}_{i}^{\top}{\hat{H}^{con}}{z}_{j}+\frac{(\hat{\Gamma}^{con})_{ij}}{n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq|P_{ij}^{*}|+\frac{c_{z}}{n}\|\hat{H}^{con}-H^{*}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}.

Further, by the constraints, we have
|(\hat{P}^{con})_{ij}|\lesssim c_{P}
. Since
\tilde{P}_{ij}
lies between
P_{ij}
and
(\hat{P}^{con})_{ij}
, we conclude that
|\tilde{P}_{ij}|\lesssim c_{P}
.
Consequently, we have
\frac{e^{\tilde{P}_{ij}}}{(1+e^{\tilde{P}_{ij}})^{2}}\geq\frac{e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}}}{(1+e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}})^{2}}
for some constant
c^{\prime\prime}
, which implies (1).

Combine (
107
) and (
110
), we have

\displaystyle L_{c}(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})-L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}+C^{\prime}\left(\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}\right)

(111)

for
C^{\prime}:=\frac{e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}}}{2(1+e^{c^{\prime\prime}c_{P}})^{2}}
. By (
106
) and (
111
), we obtain

\displaystyle C^{\prime}\left(\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\end{bmatrix}+\lambda\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}-\lambda\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}.

(112)

Recall that
\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{*}-\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}\|_{*}\geq\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}
. Consequently, we have

\displaystyle C^{\prime}\left(\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\left\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\left\|\frac{\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}}{n}\right\|_{F}^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}}{n}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Delta_{H}^{con}\\
\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\end{bmatrix}-\lambda\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}-(\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z}\nabla_{\Gamma}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\mathcal{P}^{\perp}_{Z})^{\top}\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}-\lambda\langle UV^{\top},\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}.

Recall the definition of
R
in (
84
), we further have

\displaystyle C^{\prime}\left(\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{2}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}-\langle R,\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}-\langle\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle-\lambda\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}.

(113)

By Claim
H.3
, we have

\displaystyle-\langle\mathcal{P}_{T^{\perp}}(R),\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\rangle\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{T^{\perp}}(R)\|\|\mathcal{P}_{T^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}\leq\left(1-\frac{{\epsilon}}{4}\right)\lambda\cdot\|\mathcal{P}_{T^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{*}

(114)

Combine (
113
) and (
114
) we get

\displaystyle C^{\prime}\left(\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{2}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}.

(115)

In the sequel, we deal with
\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}^{2}
. One can see that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(P_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})+P_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}))_{ii}^{2}\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(P_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}))_{ii}^{2}+2\sum_{i=1}^{n}(P_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}))_{ii}^{2}.

(116)

By Lemma
E.1
we know that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(P_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}))_{ii}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{5}\left\|P_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{5}\left\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\right\|_{F}^{2}.

(117)

On the other hand, by (
109
) we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}(P_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}))_{ii}^{2}\leq\left\|P_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq\left\|P_{\mathcal{T}^{\perp}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\right\|_{*}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{4}{\epsilon\lambda n^{5}}\right)^{2}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{72\kappa}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\ll\frac{1}{n}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}\right).

(118)

Combine (
116
), (
117
) and (
118
) we know that

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{c}}{2}\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}-\frac{2}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})_{ii}^{2}\geq\frac{\underline{c}}{3}\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{6n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}.

Combine this with (
115
), we get

\displaystyle C^{\prime\prime}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})^{\top}\Delta_{H}^{con}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{H}L_{c}(\hat{H},\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\|_{F}\left\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\right\|_{F}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(R)\|_{F}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|P_{\mathcal{T}}(\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con})\|_{F}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}\right),

where
C^{\prime\prime}:=C^{\prime}\min\{\underline{c}/3,1/6\}
. Since
\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}^{2}\geq(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F})^{2}/2
, we know that

\displaystyle\frac{C^{\prime\prime}}{2}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}\right)^{2}\leq\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}\left(\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}\right).

As a result, we get

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}\leq\frac{2}{C^{\prime\prime}}\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}.

(119)

Further by (
18
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}\lesssim n^{-5},\quad\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}\lesssim n^{-4}.

Consequently, we show that

\displaystyle\|\hat{H}^{con}-H^{*}\|_{F}\leq\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{n}<cn,

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Gamma}^{con}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}+\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\lesssim c_{41}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}<cn

as long as
n
is large enough.
In other words, the minimizer of (
105
) is in the interior of the constraint. By the convexity of (
105
), we have
(\hat{H}^{con},\hat{\Gamma}^{con})=(\hat{H}_{c},\hat{\Gamma}_{c})
.
Consequently, by (
119
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}_{c}-\hat{H}\\
\frac{1}{n}(\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\hat{H}^{con}-\hat{H}\\
\frac{1}{n}(\hat{\Gamma}^{con}-\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top})\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Delta_{H}^{con}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n}\|\Delta_{\Gamma}^{con}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{C^{\prime\prime}}\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}.

Thus, we prove Theorem
H.1
.
∎

H.3
Proofs of Theorem
3.2

Note that
\|\mathcal{P}(\nabla f(\hat{H},\hat{X},\hat{Y}))\|_{F}\lesssim n^{-5}
.
By Theorem
H.1
and Lemma
C.4
, we then have

\displaystyle\|\hat{H}_{c}-H^{*}\|_{F}\lesssim\|\hat{H}-H^{*}\|_{F}\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{n}.

Note that by Lemma
C.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{F}\leq\|\hat{X}-{X}^{*}\|_{F}\|\hat{Y}\|+\|\hat{Y}-{Y}^{*}\|_{F}\|{X}^{*}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11}.

By Lemma
C.4
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\hat{X}-{X}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|\hat{Y}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\hat{Y}-{Y}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|{X}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}c_{41}.

Further by Theorem
H.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{F}\lesssim\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{F}\lesssim\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11}

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\lesssim\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|_{\infty}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}c_{41}.

Appendix I
Proofs of Proposition
3.4
and Theorem
3.7

I.1
Proofs of Proposition
3.4

Compared to
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Proposition A.1)
, the only difference between our identifiable condition and theirs is the sign of diagonal entries of
W
. In fact, as to the proof of this condition, we only need to make a slight modification on the basis of
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Proposition A.1)
.

Assume that we have two sets of
(\Theta,\Pi,W)
and
(\tilde{\Theta},\tilde{\Pi},\tilde{W})
which satisfy Proposition
3.4
and
\Theta\Pi W\Pi^{\top}\Theta=\tilde{\Theta}\tilde{\Pi}\tilde{W}\tilde{\Pi}^{\top}\tilde{\Theta}
. According to
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Proof of Proposition A.1)
, if the row
i
of
\Pi
(or
\tilde{\Pi}
) represents a pure node, then the row
i
of
\tilde{\Pi}
(or
\Pi
) also represents a pure node, and these two sets of pure nodes are identical up to a permutation of the columns. Therefore, without loss of generality, we assume that
\Pi_{1:K,:}
and
\tilde{\Pi}_{1:K,:}
are all equal to the identity matrix. Comparing the submatrices
(\Theta\Pi W\Pi^{\top}\Theta)_{1:K,1:K}
and
(\tilde{\Theta}\tilde{\Pi}\tilde{W}\tilde{\Pi}^{\top}\tilde{\Theta})_{1:K,1:K}
, which should be identical, we get

\displaystyle\Theta_{1:K,1:K}W\Theta_{1:K,1:K}=\tilde{\Theta}_{1:K,1:K}\tilde{W}\tilde{\Theta}_{1:K,1:K}.

(120)

Particularly, we know that
\theta_{i}^{2}W_{ii}=\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{2}\tilde{W}_{ii}
for all
i\in[r]
. Since
\theta_{i}^{2},\tilde{\theta}_{i}^{2}>0
, we know that
W_{ii}
and
\tilde{W}_{ii}
must have the same sign. By Proposition
3.4
,
|W_{ii}|=|\tilde{W}_{ii}|=1
. Therefore, we know that
W_{ii}=\tilde{W}_{ii}
. This also implies
\theta_{i}=\tilde{\theta}_{i}
, and thus
\Theta_{1:K,1:K}=\tilde{\Theta}_{1:K,1:K}
. Plugging this back in (
120
), we get
W=\tilde{W}
. The rest of the proof is the same as
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Proof of Proposition A.1)
, and we finally reach
(\Theta,\Pi,W)=(\tilde{\Theta},\tilde{\Pi},\tilde{W})
. That is to say, the DCMM model
\Gamma=\Theta\Pi W\Pi^{\top}\Theta
is identifiable under the conditions in Proposition
3.4
.

I.2
Proofs of Theorem
3.7

In this section, we will frequently using
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Lemma C.2, C.3, C.4)
. Since our condition on
W^{*}
is slightly adapted from
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Proposition A.1)
, the
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Lemma C.2)
has to be modified here. We state the result we are going to use as follows.

Lemma I.1
.

Under Proposition
3.4
and Assumption
8
, we have

•

r^{-1}\left\|\theta^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim|\lambda_{1}|\lesssim\left\|\theta^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}
.

•

|\lambda_{1}|-|\lambda_{2}|\asymp|\lambda_{1}|
.

•

|\lambda_{k}|\asymp\beta_{n}r^{-1}\left\|\theta^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}
for all
1\leq k\leq r
.

Let
\hat{U}_{full}\hat{\Sigma}_{full}\hat{V}_{full}^{\top}
be the SVD of
\hat{\Gamma}_{c}
and assume the diagonal entries of
\hat{\Sigma}_{full}
are sorted in a descending order. We denote by

\displaystyle\hat{U}_{c}=(\hat{U}_{full})_{:,1:r},\quad\hat{\Sigma}_{c}=(\hat{\Sigma}_{full})_{1:r,1:r},\quad\hat{V}_{c}=(\hat{V}_{full})_{:,1:r}.

We choose the signs such that the left singular vectors
\hat{U}_{c}
are coincident with the eigenvectors associated with the largest
r
(in magnitude) eigenvalues. Also, let
\hat{U}\hat{\Sigma}\hat{V}^{\top}
be the SVD of
\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}
, where
\hat{X},\hat{Y}
are the nonconvex estimators given in Section
D
. Define

\displaystyle R_{U}={\arg\min}_{R\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-\hat{U}\right\|_{F}\text{ and }R_{V}={\arg\min}_{R\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{V}_{c}R-\hat{V}\right\|_{F}.

We begin with the following lemma.

Lemma I.2
.

Define

\displaystyle R:={\arg\min}_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}_{c}L-U^{*}\right\|_{F}.

Then it holds that

\displaystyle\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

Proof.

We define

\displaystyle R_{1}:={\arg\min}_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}L-\hat{U}_{c}R\right\|_{F},

\displaystyle R_{2}:={\arg\min}_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}R_{1}L-U^{*}\right\|_{F}.

By Weyl’s inequality and the proof of Theorem
3.2
, we have

\displaystyle|\sigma_{\min}(\hat{\Gamma})-\sigma_{\min}|\leq\|\hat{\Gamma}-{\Gamma}^{*}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11},

which implies
\sigma_{\min}(\hat{\Gamma})\geq\sigma_{\min}/2
. By Davis-Kahan Theorem, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{U}_{c}R-\hat{U}R_{1}\|_{F}=\min_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}L-\hat{U}_{c}R\right\|_{F}=\min_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}L-\hat{U}_{c}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{r}}{\sigma_{\min}(\hat{\Gamma})}\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\hat{\Gamma}\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{r}}{\sigma_{\min}}\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\hat{\Gamma}\|_{F}\lesssim_{(1)}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}}\frac{(1+e^{cc_{P}})^{2}}{\underline{c}e^{cc_{P}}}\left(1+\frac{72\kappa n}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\right)n^{-4}\lesssim n^{-5},

where (1) follows from Theorem
H.1
and (
18
). Also, by Davis-Kahan Theorem we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\right\|\lesssim\frac{\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|}{\sigma_{r}(\Gamma^{*})}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11}}{\sigma_{\min}}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\right\|\left\|U^{*}\right\|\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11}}{\sigma_{\min}}\ll\frac{1}{2}\sigma^{2}_{r}(U^{*}),

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-\hat{U}R_{1}\right\|\left\|U^{*}\right\|\lesssim n^{-5}\sigma^{2}_{r}(U^{*})

as long as
n
is large enough, by Lemma
J.2
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-\hat{U}R_{1}R_{2}\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{5\sigma^{2}_{1}(U^{*})}{\sigma^{2}_{r}(U^{*})}\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-\hat{U}R_{1}\right\|_{F}\lesssim n^{-5}.

(121)

We define
\hat{R}=R_{1}R_{2}
. It’s easy to see that

\displaystyle\hat{R}={\arg\min}_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\left\|\hat{U}L-U^{*}\right\|_{F}.

(122)

We then turn to control
\|\hat{U}\hat{R}-U^{*}\|_{2,\infty}
. By Claim
H.2
, there exists an invertible matrix
Q
such that
\hat{X}=\hat{U}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q
and (
82
) holds. By the definition of
X^{*}
, we have
X^{*}=U^{*}\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\hat{U}=\hat{X}(\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q)^{-1},\quad\hat{U}^{\top}\hat{X}=\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q

\displaystyle U^{*}=X^{*}(\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}})^{-1},\quad U^{*\top}X^{*}=\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}.

(123)

It then holds that

\displaystyle\|\hat{U}\hat{R}-U^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\hat{X}(\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q)^{-1}\hat{R}-X^{*}(\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}})^{-1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\hat{X}-X^{*})(\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}})^{-1}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\hat{X}(Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}-\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}})\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\hat{X}-X^{*}\|_{2,\infty}+\|Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}-\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\hat{X}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}c_{41}+2\|Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}-\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}},

(124)

where the last inequality follows from (
17
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\|\hat{X}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\|\hat{X}-X^{*}\|_{2,\infty}+\|{X}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\|{X}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\mu r\sigma_{\max}}{n}}.

Note that

\displaystyle\|Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}-\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}(\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q-\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}})Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q-\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}\|\|Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\|Q^{-1}\|\|\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q-\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}\|.

Since
Q
satisfies (
82
), we have
\|Q^{-1}\|\leq 2
. Moreover, we have show that
\|\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|=\sqrt{1/\sigma_{\min}(\hat{\Gamma})}\leq\sqrt{2/\sigma_{\min}}
.
Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\|Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}-\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\leq\frac{4}{\sigma_{\min}}\|\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q-\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}\|.

(125)

By (
123
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q-\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}\|

\displaystyle=\|\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{U}^{\top}\hat{X}-{U}^{*\top}{X}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{U}^{\top}-{U}^{*\top})\hat{X}\|+\|{U}^{*\top}(\hat{X}-{X}^{*})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{X}\|\|\hat{U}\hat{R}-U^{*}\|+\|\hat{X}-{X}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\|\hat{U}\hat{R}-U^{*}\|_{F}+c_{11}\sqrt{n},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
and the fact that

\displaystyle\|\hat{X}\|\leq\|\hat{X}-X^{*}\|+\|{X}^{*}\|\leq 2\|{X}^{*}\|=2\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}.

By Davis-Kahan Theorem and (
122
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{U}\hat{R}-U^{*}\|_{F}\lesssim\frac{\|\hat{X}\hat{Y}^{\top}-\Gamma^{*}\|}{\sigma_{r}(\Gamma^{*})}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11}}{\sigma_{\min}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{R}^{\top}\hat{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}Q-\Sigma^{*\frac{1}{2}}\|\lesssim 2\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}c_{11}}{\sigma_{\min}}+c_{11}\sqrt{n}\lesssim\kappa c_{11}\sqrt{n}.

(126)

Combine (
125
) and (
126
), we get

\displaystyle\|Q^{-1}\hat{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{R}-\Sigma^{*-\frac{1}{2}}\|\lesssim\frac{\kappa c_{11}\sqrt{n}}{\sigma_{\min}}.

(127)

Further by (
124
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{U}\hat{R}-U^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

Combine above inequality with (
121
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{U}_{c}R-\hat{U}\hat{R}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\hat{U}\hat{R}R-U^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-5}+\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

We then finish the proof of Lemma
I.2
.
∎

Let
\check{U}_{c}:=(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,2:r}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times(r-1)}
be the
2
-th to
r
-th column of
\hat{U}_{c}
and
\check{U}^{*}:=(U^{*})_{:,2:r}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times(r-1)}
be the
2
-th to
r
-th column of
U^{*}
. Define
\check{R}\in\mathbb{R}^{(r-1)\times(r-1)}
as the rotation matrix aligns
\check{U}_{c}
and
\check{U}^{*}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\check{R}:={\arg\min}_{L\in\mathcal{O}^{(r-1)\times(r-1)}}\left\|\check{U}_{c}L-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{F}.

Moreover, without loss of generality, we choose the direction of
(U^{*})_{:,1}
such that
(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}\geq 0
.
Then we have the following results.

Lemma I.3
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\|\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}},

\displaystyle\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}\|_{\infty}\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

Proof.

We define

\displaystyle H:=\hat{U}_{c}^{\top}U^{*},\quad\check{H}:=\check{U}_{c}^{\top}\check{U}^{*}.

According to this definition, one can see that

\displaystyle H_{2:r,2:r}=(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,2:r}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,2:r}=\check{U}_{c}^{\top}\check{U}^{*}=\check{H}.

Therefore, we can control the different between
R_{2:r,2:r}
and
\check{R}
as

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{R}\right\|\leq\left\|R_{2:r,2:r}-H_{2:r,2:r}\right\|+\left\|H_{2:r,2:r}-\check{H}\right\|+\left\|\check{H}-\check{R}\right\|\leq\left\|R-H\right\|+\left\|\check{H}-\check{R}\right\|.

By Davis-Karhan Theorem and Lemma 2 in
Yan et al., (2024)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|R-H\right\|\lesssim\left(\frac{\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{2}.

Similarly, according to Assumption
8
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\check{H}-\check{R}\right\|\lesssim\left(\frac{\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{2}.

Combine these two results we get

\displaystyle\left\|R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{R}\right\|\lesssim\left(\frac{\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{2}\lesssim\frac{c_{11}^{2}\sigma_{\max}n}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}.

(128)

On the other hand,
\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*}
can be written as

\displaystyle\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*}=\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}+\check{U}_{c}(\check{R}-R_{2:r,2:r}).

(129)

It remains to control
\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}
. Notice that

\displaystyle(\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*})_{:,2:r}=\hat{U}_{c}R_{:,2:r}-U^{*}_{:,2:r}=(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}R_{1,2:r}+\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}.

Therefore,
\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}
can be controlled as

\displaystyle\left\|\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*})_{:,2:r}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}R_{1,2:r}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|R_{1,2:r}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{n}}\left\|R_{1,2:r}\right\|_{2}.

(130)

The term
\left\|R_{1,2:r}\right\|_{2}
can be further controlled as

\displaystyle\left\|R_{1,2:r}\right\|_{2}=\sqrt{\left\|R_{:,2:r}\right\|_{F}^{2}-\left\|R_{2:r,2:r}\right\|_{F}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r-1-\left(\left\|\check{R}\right\|_{F}-\left\|\check{R}-R_{2:r,2:r}\right\|_{F}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\sqrt{\left\|\check{R}-R_{2:r,2:r}\right\|_{F}}\lesssim\frac{c_{11}r^{3/4}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}}{\sigma_{\min}}.

(131)

Plugging (
131
) in (
130
) we get

\displaystyle\left\|\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

Combing this with (
128
) and (
129
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\check{U}_{c}(\check{R}-R_{2:r,2:r})\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|\check{U}_{c}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|\check{R}-R_{2:r,2:r}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\check{U}_{c}R_{2:r,2:r}-\check{U}^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{n}}+\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}\right)\frac{c_{11}^{2}\sigma_{\max}n}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

Now we turn to control
\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}\|_{\infty}
. Similarly, one can see that

\displaystyle\left|R_{1,1}-1\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|R_{1,1}-H_{1,1}\right|+\left|H_{1,1}-(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}\right|+\left|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}-1\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|R-H\right\|+\left|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}-1\right|\lesssim\frac{c_{11}^{2}\sigma_{\max}n}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}.

On the other hand,
(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}
can be decomposed into

\displaystyle(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}

\displaystyle=(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}R_{1,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}+(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(1-R_{1,1})

\displaystyle=(\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*})_{:,1}-(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,2:r}R_{2:r,1}+(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(1-R_{1,1}).

Since
\|R_{2:r,1}\|_{2}^{2}=r-1-\|R_{2:r,2:r}\|_{F}^{2}=\|R_{1,2:r}\|_{2}^{2}
, the inequality (
131
) also applies to
\|R_{2:r,1}\|_{2}
. Therefore,
\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}\|_{\infty}
can be controlled as

\displaystyle\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*})_{:,1}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,2:r}R_{2:r,1}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(1-R_{1,1})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\hat{U}_{c}R-U^{*}\right\|_{2,\infty}+\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,2:r}\right\|_{2,\infty}\left\|R_{2:r,1}\right\|_{2}+\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}\right\|_{\infty}|1-R_{1,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}.

∎

As a direct corollary of Lemma
I.3
, we have the following result.

Corollary I.4
.

\forall i\in[n]
, it holds that

\displaystyle(\hat{U}_{c})_{1,i}\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

Proof.

(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Lemma C.3)
shows that
(U^{*})_{1,i}\asymp 1/\sqrt{n}
. Combine this with Lemma
I.3
, as long as

\displaystyle\frac{c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}}}\ll\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},

we have
(\hat{U}_{c})_{1,i}\asymp 1/\sqrt{n}
.
∎

Then we are ready to control the estimation error of the eigen ratio
\hat{r}_{i}
.

Lemma I.5
.

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|\check{R}^{\top}\hat{r}_{i}-r_{i}^{*}\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}c_{41}+\frac{\mu r\sqrt{\kappa\sigma_{\max}n}}{\sigma_{\min}}c_{11}+\frac{c_{11}\mu r^{7/4}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}}{\sigma_{\min}}.

Proof.

By definition we can write

\displaystyle\check{R}^{\top}\hat{r}_{i}-r_{i}^{*}=\frac{(\check{U}_{c}\check{R})_{i,:}^{\top}}{(\hat{U}_{c})_{i,1}}-\frac{(\check{U}^{*})_{i,:}^{\top}}{(U^{*})_{i,1}}=\frac{(\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*})_{i,:}^{\top}}{(\hat{U}_{c})_{i,1}}+\frac{(U^{*})_{i,1}-(\hat{U}_{c})_{i,1}}{(\hat{U}_{c})_{i,1}(U^{*})_{i,1}}(\check{U}^{*})_{i,:}^{\top}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\hat{r}_{i}-r_{i}^{*}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}}{|(\hat{U}_{c})_{i,1}|}+\frac{\|(U^{*})_{:,1}-(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}\|_{\infty}}{|(\hat{U}_{c})_{i,1}(U^{*})_{i,1}|}\|\check{U}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{n}\|\check{U}_{c}\check{R}-\check{U}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}+n\|(U^{*})_{:,1}-(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}.

∎

By Lemma
I.5
, the eigen ratio
r_{i}^{*}
can be estimated uniformly well in the sense that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|\check{R}^{\top}\hat{r}_{i}-r_{i}^{*}\|_{2}\lesssim\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}.

(132)

Recall the definition of efficient vertex-hunting algorithms, we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq\ell\leq r}\|\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\|_{2}\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}-r_{i}^{*}\|_{2}\lesssim\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}.

(133)

We then prove Theorem
3.7
in the following.

Proof of Theorem
3.7
.

Note that

\displaystyle\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}v_{1}^{*}&\dots&v_{r}^{*}\\
1&\dots&1\end{bmatrix}}_{=:Q}w^{*}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}r^{*}_{i}\\
1\end{bmatrix},\quad\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}\check{R}\hat{v}_{1}&\dots&\check{R}\hat{v}_{r}\\
1&\dots&1\end{bmatrix}}_{=:\hat{Q}}\hat{w}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}\\
1\end{bmatrix}.

The following claim is from
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, (C.26))
.

Claim I.6
.

Under Assumption
8
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|Q\|\lesssim\sqrt{r}\text{ and }\|Q^{-1}\|\lesssim\sqrt{1/r}.

By Claim
I.6
, we have
\sigma_{r}(Q)\gtrsim\sqrt{r}
.
By Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\sigma_{r}(\hat{Q})-\sigma_{r}(Q)|

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{Q}-Q\|\leq\|\hat{Q}-Q\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{r}\max_{1\leq\ell\leq r}\|\check{R}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}

\displaystyle\ll\sqrt{r}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\hat{Q}^{-1}\|=\frac{1}{\sigma_{r}(\hat{Q})}\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{r}({Q})}\lesssim\sqrt{1/r}.

(134)

Note that

\displaystyle\hat{w}_{i}-w^{*}_{i}

\displaystyle=\hat{Q}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}\\
1\end{bmatrix}-Q^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}r^{*}_{i}\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\hat{Q}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}-r^{*}_{i}\\
0\end{bmatrix}-\hat{Q}^{-1}(\hat{Q}-Q)Q^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}r^{*}_{i}\\
1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\hat{Q}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}-r^{*}_{i}\\
0\end{bmatrix}-\hat{Q}^{-1}(\hat{Q}-Q)w^{*}_{i}.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{w}_{i}-w^{*}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{Q}^{-1}\|\left(\|\check{R}\hat{u}_{i}-u^{*}_{i}\|_{2}+\|(\hat{Q}-Q)w^{*}_{i}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{1/r}\left(\|\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}-r^{*}_{i}\|_{2}+\|(\hat{Q}-Q)w^{*}_{i}\|_{2}\right),

where the last inequality follows from (
134
).
Note that

\displaystyle\|(\hat{Q}-Q)w^{*}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum^{r}_{\ell=1}w^{*}_{i}(\ell)\begin{bmatrix}\check{R}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{r}_{\ell=1}w^{*}_{i}(\ell)\|\check{R}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq\ell\leq r}\|\check{R}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\|_{2},

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
\sum^{r}_{\ell=1}w^{*}_{i}(\ell)=1
. Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\hat{w}_{i}-w^{*}_{i}\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{1/r}\left(\|\check{R}\hat{r}_{i}-r^{*}_{i}\|_{2}+\max_{1\leq\ell\leq r}\|\check{R}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\|_{2}\right).

Further by (
132
) and (
133
), we obtain

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|\hat{w}_{i}-w^{*}_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu n}{\sigma_{\min}}}.

(135)

Next, let’s control
|(\hat{b}_{1}(l))^{-1}-(b^{*}_{1}(l))^{-1}|
. By definition we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\hat{b}_{1}(l)}-\frac{1}{b^{*}_{1}(l)}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sqrt{\left|\hat{\lambda}_{1}+\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}\right|}-\sqrt{\left|\lambda_{1}+v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\left|\hat{\lambda}_{1}+\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}\right|-\left|\lambda_{1}+v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|}{\sqrt{\left|\hat{\lambda}_{1}+\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}\right|}+\sqrt{\left|\lambda_{1}+v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left|\hat{\lambda}_{1}+\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}-\lambda_{1}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|}{\sqrt{\lambda_{1}+v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}}}

\displaystyle\leq b_{1}^{*}(\ell)\left(\left|\hat{\lambda}_{1}-\lambda_{1}\right|+\left|\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|\right).

(136)

By
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Eq. (C.22))
we know that
b_{1}^{*}(\ell)\asymp(\sqrt{n}\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*})^{-1}
. On the other hand, by Weyl’s inequality we know that

\displaystyle\left|\hat{\lambda}_{1}-\lambda_{1}\right|\leq\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}n}.

(137)

It remains to control
\left|\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|
. Define

\displaystyle\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}={\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r}),\overline{\Lambda}={\sf diag}({\lambda}_{2},\ldots,{\lambda}_{r}),

we can write

\displaystyle\left|\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}\overline{\Lambda}v^{*}_{\ell}\right|=\left|(\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell})^{\top}\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}\overline{\Lambda}v^{*}_{\ell}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|(\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell})^{\top}\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}\right|+\left|v_{\ell}^{*\top}\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}\overline{\Lambda}\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|v_{\ell}^{*\top}\overline{\Lambda}\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}\overline{\Lambda}v_{\ell}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\left\|\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\hat{v}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\hat{v}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\left\|v_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\overline{\Lambda}\right\|+\left\|v_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\left\|\overline{\Lambda}\right\|\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}.

(138)

Furthermore, we write

\displaystyle\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\overline{\Lambda}=\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}+\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}-\overline{\Lambda}.

(139)

The first term on the RHS can be controlled as

\displaystyle\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}+\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}-\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}\right\|+\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}-\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}\right\|\leq 2\left\|\check{R}-\check{H}\right\|\left\|\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{2}\sigma_{2}(\hat{\Gamma}_{c}).

(140)

The second term can be controlled as

\displaystyle\left\|\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}-\overline{\Lambda}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\check{U}^{*\top}\check{U}_{c}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{U}_{c}^{\top}\check{U}^{*}-\overline{\Lambda}\right\|=\left\|\check{U}^{*\top}(\check{U}_{c}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{U}_{c}^{\top}-\check{U}^{*}\overline{\Lambda}\check{U}^{*\top})\check{U}^{*}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\check{U}_{c}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{U}_{c}^{\top}-\check{U}^{*}\overline{\Lambda}\check{U}^{*\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\hat{\lambda}_{1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-\sum_{k=r+1}^{n}\hat{\lambda}_{k}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,k}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,k}^{\top}-\left(\Gamma^{*}-\lambda_{1}(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\left\|\sum_{k=r+1}^{n}\hat{\lambda}_{k}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,k}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,k}^{\top}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\lambda}_{1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-\lambda_{1}(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\sigma_{r+1}(\hat{\Gamma}_{c})+\left\|\hat{\lambda}_{1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-\lambda_{1}(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\lambda}_{1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-\lambda_{1}(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right\|.

(141)

The last term can be further controlled by

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\lambda}_{1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-\lambda_{1}(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(\hat{\lambda}_{1}-\lambda_{1})(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}\right\|+\left\|\lambda_{1}\left((\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq|\hat{\lambda}_{1}-\lambda_{1}|+\lambda_{1}\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}-(U^{*})_{:,1}(U^{*})_{:,1}^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=|\hat{\lambda}_{1}-\lambda_{1}|+\lambda_{1}\sqrt{1-\left((\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\lambda_{1}\sqrt{2-2(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\lambda_{1}\sqrt{\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}\|_{2}+\|(U^{*})_{:,1}\|_{2}-2(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}^{\top}(U^{*})_{:,1}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\lambda_{1}\left\|(\hat{U}_{c})_{:,1}-(U^{*})_{:,1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|+\lambda_{1}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|}{\lambda_{1}-\sigma_{2}(\Gamma^{*})}\lesssim\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|.

Combine this with (
141
) we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\check{H}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{H}-\overline{\Lambda}\right\|\lesssim\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|.

Plugging this and (
140
) in (
139
) we get

\displaystyle\left\|\check{R}^{\top}\overline{\Lambda}^{\prime}\check{R}-\overline{\Lambda}\right\|\lesssim\left(\frac{\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\|}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)^{2}\sigma_{2}(\hat{\Gamma}_{c})+\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|\lesssim\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|

as long as
\sigma_{\min}\gtrsim\kappa\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|
.

Before we go back to (
138
), we need to control
\left\|v_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}
and
\left\|\hat{v}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}
. By Assumption
4
and
(
Jin et al.,, 2023
, Lemma C.3)
, it can be controlled as

\displaystyle\left\|v_{\ell}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\mu r/n}}{1/\sqrt{n}}=\sqrt{\mu r}.

And, as long as

\displaystyle\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{n}{\sigma_{\min}}}\ll 1,

we also have
\left\|\hat{v}_{\ell}\right\|_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\mu r}
. As a result, from (
138
) we know that

\displaystyle\left|\hat{v}_{\ell}^{\top}{\sf diag}(\hat{\lambda}_{2},\ldots,\hat{\lambda}_{r})\hat{v}_{\ell}-v_{\ell}^{*\top}{\sf diag}(\lambda_{2},\ldots,\lambda_{r})v^{*}_{\ell}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\sqrt{\mu r}\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}+\mu r\left\|\hat{\Gamma}_{c}-\Gamma^{*}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu r\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\kappa n\sigma_{\max}}.

Combine this with (
137
) and plug them back in (
136
) we get

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\hat{b}_{1}(l)}-\frac{1}{b^{*}_{1}(l)}\right|\lesssim b_{1}^{*}(\ell)\mu r\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\kappa n\sigma_{\max}}.

By Lemma
I.1
we know that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\lesssim(\sqrt{n}\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*})^{2}\asymp\frac{1}{b_{1}^{*2}(\ell)}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\hat{b}_{1}(l)}-\frac{1}{b^{*}_{1}(l)}\right|\lesssim\mu r\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\kappa n}.

Combine this with (
135
), we are able to control
\tilde{\pi}_{i}(\ell)-\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}(\ell)
, where
\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}(\ell)
is defined as

\displaystyle\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}(\ell):=\frac{w^{*}_{i}(\ell)}{b^{*}_{1}(\ell)},\quad\forall i\in[n],\ell\in[r].

Since
\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}(\ell)\geq 0
, one can see that

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}-\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\left|\max\left\{\frac{\hat{w}_{i}(\ell)}{\hat{b}_{1}(\ell)},0\right\}-\frac{w^{*}_{i}(\ell)}{b^{*}_{1}(\ell)}\right|\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\left|\frac{\hat{w}_{i}(\ell)}{\hat{b}_{1}(\ell)}-\frac{w^{*}_{i}(\ell)}{b^{*}_{1}(\ell)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\min_{\ell\in[r]}\hat{b}_{1}(\ell)}\left\|\hat{w}_{i}-w_{i}^{*}\right\|_{1}+\left\|w^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}\max_{\ell\in[r]}\left|\frac{1}{\hat{b}_{1}(l)}-\frac{1}{b^{*}_{1}(l)}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*}\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}

\displaystyle+\mu r\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\sqrt{\kappa n}.

(142)

Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}^{*}\right\|_{1}=\left\|\frac{\tilde{\pi}_{i}}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}\right\|_{1}}-\frac{\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}\right\|_{1}\left|\frac{1}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}\right\|_{1}}-\frac{1}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}\right|+\frac{\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}-\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left|\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}\right\|_{1}-\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}\right|}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}+\frac{\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}-\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}\leq 2\frac{\left\|\tilde{\pi}_{i}-\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}{\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}}.

(143)

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{\pi}^{*}_{i}\right\|_{1}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\frac{w^{*}_{i}(\ell)}{b^{*}_{1}(\ell)}\asymp\sqrt{n}\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*}\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}w^{*}_{i}(\ell)=\sqrt{n}\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*},

by (
142
) and (
143
), we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}^{*}\right\|_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu rn}{\sigma_{\min}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\kappa}\mu r}{\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*}}\right).

Again by Lemma
I.1
we know that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\asymp\beta_{n}r^{-1}(\sqrt{n}\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*})^{2}.

Therefore, we know that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}^{*}\right\|_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(c_{41}+c_{11}\kappa^{1.5}\sqrt{\mu r}+c_{11}\sqrt{\mu\kappa}r^{5/4}\right)\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu}{\beta_{n}}}+\sqrt{\kappa}\mu\right)\frac{r}{\bar{\theta}_{2}^{*}}.

∎

Appendix J
Technical lemmas

Lemma J.1
.

For matrix
A\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}\times m},B\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}\times m}
, we have

\displaystyle\max\{\|A\|,\|B\|\}\leq\left\|\begin{bmatrix}A\\
B\end{bmatrix}\right\|\leq\|A\|+\|B\|.

Proof of Lemma
J.1
.

Given
A\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}\times m},B\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}\times m}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}A\\
B\end{bmatrix}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}+n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2},\|v\|_{2}\leq 1}v^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}A\\
B\end{bmatrix}u

\displaystyle=\sup_{v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}},v_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\sqrt{\|v_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|v_{2}\|_{2}^{2}}\leq 1}v_{1}^{\top}Au+v_{2}^{\top}Bu

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}},v_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2},\|v_{1}\|_{2},\|v_{2}\|_{2}\leq 1}v_{1}^{\top}Au+v_{2}^{\top}Bu

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2}\|v_{1}\|_{2}\leq 1}v_{1}^{\top}Au+\sup_{v_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2},\|v_{2}\|_{2}\leq 1}v_{2}^{\top}Bu

\displaystyle=\|A\|_{2}+\|B\|_{2}.

Given
A\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}\times m},B\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}\times m}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}A\\
B\end{bmatrix}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}+n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2},\|v\|_{2}\leq 1}v^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}A\\
B\end{bmatrix}u

\displaystyle=\sup_{v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}},v_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\sqrt{\|v_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|v_{2}\|_{2}^{2}}\leq 1}v_{1}^{\top}Au+v_{2}^{\top}Bu

\displaystyle\geq\sup_{v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}},v_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{2}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\sqrt{\|v_{1}\|_{2}^{2}+\|v_{2}\|_{2}^{2}}\leq 1,v_{2}=0}v_{1}^{\top}Au+v_{2}^{\top}Bu

\displaystyle=\sup_{v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n_{1}},u\in\mathbb{R}^{m},\|u\|_{2}\leq 1,\|v_{1}\|_{2}<=1}v_{1}^{\top}Au=\|A\|_{2}.

∎

Lemma J.2
.

Suppose
F_{1},F_{2},F_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{2n\times r}
are three matrices such that

\|F_{1}-F_{0}\|\|F_{0}\|\leq\sigma_{r}^{2}(F_{0})/2\quad\text{and}\quad\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|\|F_{0}\|\leq\sigma_{r}^{2}(F_{0})/4,

where
\sigma_{i}(A)
stands for the
i
-th largest singular value of
A
. Denote

\mathbf{R}_{1}\triangleq\arg\min_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\|F_{1}\mathbf{R}-F_{0}\|_{F}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbf{R}_{2}\triangleq\arg\min_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r\times r}}\|F_{2}\mathbf{R}-F_{0}\|_{F}.

Then the following two inequalities hold:

\|F_{1}\mathbf{R}_{1}-F_{2}\mathbf{R}_{2}\|\leq\frac{5\sigma_{1}^{2}(F_{0})}{\sigma_{r}^{2}(F_{0})}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|\quad\text{and}\quad\|F_{1}\mathbf{R}_{1}-F_{2}\mathbf{R}_{2}\|_{F}\leq\frac{5\sigma_{1}^{2}(F_{0})}{\sigma_{r}^{2}(F_{0})}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|_{F}.

Proof of Lemma
J.2
.

See Lemma 37 in
Ma et al., (2018)
.
∎

Lemma J.3
.

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. We then have

\displaystyle\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\right\|_{F}\leq 5\kappa\left\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\right\|_{F}.

Proof of Lemma
J.3
.

By Lemma
C.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|\|F^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{r}^{2}(F^{*})/2.

Note that

\displaystyle\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\|_{F}+\|F^{*}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\|_{F}+\|F^{*}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}

(by the definition of
R^{t,(m)}
)

\displaystyle\leq\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}+2\|F^{*}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq c_{21}+2c_{11}\sqrt{n}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|\|F^{*}\|\leq\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}\|F^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{r}^{2}(F^{*})/4.

Then by Lemma
J.2
with
F_{0}=F^{*},F_{1}=F^{t}R^{t},F_{2}=F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5\sigma_{1}^{2}(F^{*})}{\sigma_{r}^{2}(F^{*})}\left\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\frac{5\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}}\left\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\right\|_{F}.

∎

Lemma J.4
.

Suppose Lemma
C.1
-Lemma
C.5
hold for the
t
-th iteration. We then have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}/2\leq\sigma_{\min}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)\leq\sigma_{\max}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)\leq 2\sigma_{\max}.

Proof of Lemma
J.4
.

By Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{\min}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)-\sigma_{\min}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sigma_{\min}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)-\sigma_{\min}\left(Y^{*T}Y^{*}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*T}Y^{*}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right\|\left(\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right\|+\left\|Y^{*}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right\|\left(\left\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\right\|+2\left\|Y^{*}\right\|\right).

Note that

\displaystyle\|Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-Y^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|F^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|+\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq 5\kappa\|F^{t,(m)}O^{t,(m)}-F^{t}R^{t}\|_{F}+\|F^{t}R^{t}-F^{*}\|

(by Lemma
J.3
)

\displaystyle\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{n}

and
\|Y^{*}\|=\sqrt{\sigma_{\max}}
.
We then have

\displaystyle\left|\sigma_{\min}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)-\sigma_{\min}\right|\lesssim c_{11}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\max}}\leq\sigma_{\min}/2,

which implies

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)\geq\sigma_{\min}/2.

Similarly, we can show that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}\left(\left(Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)^{T}Y^{t,(m)}R^{t,(m)}\right)\leq 2\sigma_{\max}.

∎

Lemma J.5
.

Let
S\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
be a nonsingular matrix. Then for any matrix
K\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
with
\|K\|\leq\sigma_{\min}(S)
, one has

\|\text{sgn}(S+K)-\text{sgn}(S)\|\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{r-1}(S)+\sigma_{r}(S)}\|K\|,

where
\text{sgn}(\cdot)
denotes the matrix sign function, i.e.
\text{sgn}(A)=UV^{\top}
for a matrix
A
with SVD
U\Sigma V^{\top}
.

Proof.

See Lemma 36 in
Ma et al., (2018)
.
∎

Lemma J.6
.

Let
U\Sigma V^{\top}
be the SVD of a rank-
r
matrix
XY^{\top}
with
X,Y\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
. Then there exists an invertible matrix
{Q}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
such that
X=U\Sigma^{1/2}{Q}
and
Y=V\Sigma^{1/2}{Q}^{-T}
. In addition, one has

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{Q}-\Sigma_{{Q}}^{-1}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma)}\|X^{\top}X-Y^{\top}Y\|_{F},

(140)

where
U_{Q}\Sigma_{Q}V_{Q}^{\top}
is the SVD of
{Q}
. In particular, if
X
and
Y
have balanced scale, i.e.,
X^{\top}X-Y^{\top}Y=0
, then
{Q}
must be a rotation matrix.

Proof.

See Lemma 20 in
Ma et al., (2018)
.
∎
